### [52] [DeepER-Med: Advancing Deep Evidence-Based Research in Medicine Through Agentic AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15456)
*Zhizheng Wang,Chih-Hsuan Wei,Joey Chan,Robert Leaman,Chi-Ping Day,Chuan Wu,Mark A Knepper,Antolin Serrano Farias,Jordina Rincon-Torroella,Hasan Slika,Betty Tyler,Ryan Huu-Tuan Nguyen,Asmita Indurkar,Mélanie Hébert,Shubo Tian,Lauren He,Noor Naffakh,Aseem Aseem,Nicholas Wan,Emily Y Chew,Tiarnan D L Keenan,Zhiyong Lu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: DeepER-Med是一个用于医学领域的深度循证研究框架，旨在解决现有AI研究系统缺乏可检验的证据评估标准的问题。该框架包含三个模块（研究规划、Agent协作、证据综合），并在100个专家级医学研究问题数据集上进行评估，表现优于现有生产级平台，在8个真实临床案例中有7个的结论与临床建议一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前AI医疗系统缺乏明确、可检验的证据评估标准，导致错误累积风险高，研究人员和临床医生难以评估输出可靠性。同时，现有基准测试很少评估复杂真实医学问题的表现。

Method: DeepER-Med将深度医学研究框架化为三个模块的工作流程：研究规划、Agent协作、证据综合。评估数据集DeepER-MedQA包含100个由11位生物医学专家策划的专家级研究问题。

Result: DeepER-Med在多个标准上优于现有生产级平台，包括生成新颖科学洞察的能力。8个真实临床案例中，人类临床医生评估表明其结论在7个案例中与临床建议一致。

Conclusion: DeepER-Med展现了AI系统在医学研究和临床决策支持方面的潜力，能够提供可检验、透明的高质量医学研究输出。

Abstract: Trustworthiness and transparency are essential for the clinical adoption of artificial intelligence (AI) in healthcare and biomedical research. Recent deep research systems aim to accelerate evidence-grounded scientific discovery by integrating AI agents with multi-hop information retrieval, reasoning, and synthesis. However, most existing systems lack explicit and inspectable criteria for evidence appraisal, creating a risk of compounding errors and making it difficult for researchers and clinicians to assess the reliability of their outputs. In parallel, current benchmarking approaches rarely evaluate performance on complex, real-world medical questions. Here, we introduce DeepER-Med, a Deep Evidence-based Research framework for Medicine with an agentic AI system. DeepER-Med frames deep medical research as an explicit and inspectable workflow of evidence-based generation, consisting of three modules: research planning, agentic collaboration, and evidence synthesis. To support realistic evaluation, we also present DeepER-MedQA, an evidence-grounded dataset comprising 100 expert-level research questions derived from authentic medical research scenarios and curated by a multidisciplinary panel of 11 biomedical experts. Expert manual evaluation demonstrates that DeepER-Med consistently outperforms widely used production-grade platforms across multiple criteria, including the generation of novel scientific insights. We further demonstrate the practical utility of DeepER-Med through eight real-world clinical cases. Human clinician assessment indicates that DeepER-Med's conclusions align with clinical recommendations in seven cases, highlighting its potential for medical research and decision support.

</details>


### [53] [Preregistered Belief Revision Contracts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15558)
*Saad Alqithami*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出PBRC（预注册信念修订契约）协议，通过将开放通信与可接受的认知变化严格分离，防止多智能体系统中的从众效应和错误级联。证明了在保守回退契约下，社会性轮次无法增加置信度，且纯从众驱动的错误-但-确定级联不可能发生。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多智能体 deliberative 系统交互虽能提升性能，但也会产生危险的从众效应：智能体可能将一致性、置信度、威望或多数视为证据，导致高置信度收敛于错误结论，即"错误但确定"(wrong-but-sure) 级联。

Method: 提出 PBRC 协议机制：公开固定一阶证据触发器、可接受的修订算子、优先级规则和回退策略。非回退步骤仅在引用预注册触发器并提供非空外部验证证据令牌集合时接受。引入配套的契约动态认知逻辑指定轨迹不变量。

Result: (a) 在保守回退的证据契约下，社会性轮次无法增加置信度，无法产生纯从众驱动的错误级联；(b) 可审计触发协议允许保持信念轨迹和规范审计痕迹的 PBRC 范式；(c) 强制执行确保任何顶级假设变更可归因于具体验证的见证集；(d) 强制轨迹仅依赖令牌暴露轨迹，在泛洪传播下由截断可达性精确刻画，给出通用证据闭包的直径界。

Conclusion: PBRC 通过协议级机制实现了可审计性和认知问责制，仿真表明其在级联抑制、可审计性和鲁棒性-活性权衡方面的有效性。该框架为 deliberative 多智能体系统的安全性提供了形式化保证。

Abstract: Deliberative multi-agent systems allow agents to exchange messages and revise beliefs over time. While this interaction is meant to improve performance, it can also create dangerous conformity effects: agreement, confidence, prestige, or majority size may be treated as if they were evidence, producing high-confidence convergence to false conclusions. To address this, we introduce PBRC (Preregistered Belief Revision Contracts), a protocol-level mechanism that strictly separates open communication from admissible epistemic change. A PBRC contract publicly fixes first-order evidence triggers, admissible revision operators, a priority rule, and a fallback policy. A non-fallback step is accepted only when it cites a preregistered trigger and provides a nonempty witness set of externally validated evidence tokens. This ensures that every substantive belief change is both enforceable by a router and auditable after the fact. In this paper, (a) we prove that under evidential contracts with conservative fallback, social-only rounds cannot increase confidence and cannot generate purely conformity-driven wrong-but-sure cascades. (b) We show that auditable trigger protocols admit evidential PBRC normal forms that preserve belief trajectories and canonicalized audit traces. (c) We demonstrate that sound enforcement yields epistemic accountability: any change of top hypothesis is attributable to a concrete validated witness set. For token-invariant contracts, (d) we prove that enforced trajectories depend only on token-exposure traces; under flooding dissemination, these traces are characterized exactly by truncated reachability, giving tight diameter bounds for universal evidence closure. Finally, we introduce a companion contractual dynamic doxastic logic to specify trace invariants, and provide simulations illustrating cascade suppression, auditability, and robustness-liveness trade-offs.

</details>


### [54] [GIST: Multimodal Knowledge Extraction and Spatial Grounding via Intelligent Semantic Topology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15495)
*Shivendra Agrawal,Bradley Hayes*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Summary generation failed


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: Navigating complex, densely packed environments like retail stores, warehouses, and hospitals poses a significant spatial grounding challenge for humans and embodied AI. In these spaces, dense visual features quickly become stale given the quasi-static nature of items, and long-tail semantic distributions challenge traditional computer vision. While Vision-Language Models (VLMs) help assistive systems navigate semantically-rich spaces, they still struggle with spatial grounding in cluttered environments. We present GIST (Grounded Intelligent Semantic Topology), a multimodal knowledge extraction pipeline that transforms a consumer-grade mobile point cloud into a semantically annotated navigation topology. Our architecture distills the scene into a 2D occupancy map, extracts its topological layout, and overlays a lightweight semantic layer via intelligent keyframe and semantic selection. We demonstrate the versatility of this structured spatial knowledge through critical downstream Human-AI interaction tasks: (1) an intent-driven Semantic Search engine that actively infers categorical alternatives and zones when exact matches fail; (2) a one-shot Semantic Localizer achieving a 1.04 m top-5 mean translation error; (3) a Zone Classification module that segments the walkable floor plan into high-level semantic regions; and (4) a Visually-Grounded Instruction Generator that synthesizes optimal paths into egocentric, landmark-rich natural language routing. In multi-criteria LLM evaluations, GIST outperforms sequence-based instruction generation baselines. Finally, an in-situ formative evaluation (N=5) yields an 80% navigation success rate relying solely on verbal cues, validating the system's capacity for universal design.

</details>


### [55] [Weak-Link Optimization for Multi-Agent Reasoning and Collaboration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15972)
*Haoyu Bian,Chaoning Zhang,Jiaquan Zhang,Xingyao Li,Yuanfang Guo,Wei Dong,Yang Yang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: WORC是一个基于弱链接原则的多智能体推理优化框架，通过元学习定位弱智能体并分配额外推理预算来补偿其可靠性不足，在保证准确率的同时增强了系统稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM多智能体框架存在推理不稳定性问题，个体智能体错误通过协作被放大；现有研究主要关注增强高能力智能体或抑制不可靠输出，而对性能瓶颈智能体的系统性识别与强化关注不足。

Method: 两阶段工作流：①弱智能体定位阶段：构建任务特征，利用群智能算法(SIAs)识别最优配置训练元学习权重预测器，实现零样本映射特征→性能权重，识别最低权重智能体为弱智能体；②弱链接优化阶段：不确定性驱动分配策略为弱智能体分配额外推理预算，预测权重越低，重复采样配额越大。

Result: 在推理基准测试中平均准确率达82.2%，同时提升了框架稳定性和跨架构泛化能力。

Conclusion: WORC通过补偿性策略（而非强化优势）增强多智能体系统鲁棒性，弱链接优化是一种更系统性的方法。

Abstract: LLM-driven multi-agent frameworks address complex reasoning tasks through multi-role collaboration. However, existing approaches often suffer from reasoning instability, where individual agent errors are amplified through collaboration, undermining overall performance. Current research mainly focuses on enhancing high-capability agents or suppressing unreliable outputs to improve framework effectiveness, while systematic identification and reinforcement of performance-limiting agents receive less attention. To address this gap, we propose WORC, a \underline{w}eak-link \underline{o}ptimization framework for multi-agent \underline{r}easoning and \underline{c}ollaboration, grounded in the weak-link principle. WORC follows a two-stage workflow. In the weak agent localization stage, task features are constructed, and a meta-learning-based weight predictor trained on optimal configurations identified by swarm intelligence algorithms (SIAs) enables zero-shot mapping from these features to agent performance weights, where the agent with the lowest predicted weight is identified as the weak agent. In the weak-link optimization stage, an uncertainty-driven allocation strategy assigns additional reasoning budgets to weak agents, with lower predicted weights leading to larger repeated-sampling quotas to compensate for reliability deficiencies. Experimental results show that WORC achieves an average accuracy of 82.2\% on reasoning benchmarks while improving framework stability and cross-architecture generalization, suggesting that compensating for weak links, rather than reinforcing strengths alone, enhances the robustness of multi-agent systems.

</details>


### [56] [SocialGrid: A Benchmark for Planning and Social Reasoning in Embodied Multi-Agent Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16022)
*Hikaru Shindo,Hanzhao Lin,Lukas Helff,Patrick Schramowski,Kristian Kersting*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: SocialGrid是一个用于评估LLM智能体在具身多智能体场景中社会推理能力的评估框架，揭示了当前最强开源模型在规划、任务执行和社会推理方面的显著不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着大语言模型从文本处理器向自主智能体演进，在具身多智能体环境中评估其社会推理能力变得至关重要。然而现有评估存在缺陷：导航能力不足会混淆对社会智能的评估，且缺乏对欺骗检测等核心社会推理能力的专项测试。

Method: 提出了SocialGrid，一个受Among Us启发的具身多智能体环境，包含Planning Oracle模块用于隔离规划能力与社会推理，提供自动故障分析和细粒度指标，并建立基于Elo评分的竞争性排行榜进行对抗性联赛评估。

Result: 实验表明，即使是最强的开源模型（GPT-OSS-120B）在任务完成和规划任务中的准确率也不足60%，且在欺骗检测任务中表现为接近随机猜测的水平，暴露了模型依赖浅层启发式规则而非积累行为证据的深层缺陷。

Conclusion: SocialGrid是一个有效的LLM多智能体社会推理评估框架，能够诊断和量化模型缺陷。未来工作方向包括通过Planning Oracle隔离社会推理缺陷，以及建立竞争性排行榜来促进对抗性联赛学习。

Abstract: As Large Language Models (LLMs) transition from text processors to autonomous agents, evaluating their social reasoning in embodied multi-agent settings becomes critical. We introduce SocialGrid, an embodied multi-agent environment inspired by Among Us that evaluates LLM agents on planning, task execution, and social reasoning. Our evaluations reveal that even the strongest open model (GPT-OSS-120B) achieves below 60% accuracy in task completion and planning, with agents getting stuck in repetitive behaviors or failing to navigate basic obstacles. Since poor navigation confounds evaluation of social intelligence, SocialGrid offers an optional Planning Oracle to isolate social reasoning from planning deficits. While planning assistance improves task completion, social reasoning remains a bottleneck: agents fail to detect deception at near-random chance regardless of scale, relying on shallow heuristics rather than accumulating behavioral evidence. SocialGrid provides automatic failure analysis and fine-grained metrics, enabling developers to diagnose and improve their agents. We also establish a competitive leaderboard using Elo ratings from adversarial league play.

</details>


### [57] [Subliminal Transfer of Unsafe Behaviors in AI Agent Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15559)
*Jacob Dang,Brian Y. Xie,Omar G. Younis*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Unsafe agent behaviors can transfer subliminally through model distillation even when explicit deletion keywords are filtered, demonstrating that behavioral biases are implicitly encoded in trajectory dynamics and cannot be prevented by surface-level data sanitization.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Prior work shows language models can transmit semantic traits through semantically unrelated data, but it is unknown whether behavioral traits can transfer in agentic systems where policies learn from trajectories rather than static text — a critical safety concern.

Method: Two complementary settings: (1) API-style tool interface where teacher agent with deletion bias is distilled into student using safe task trajectories with keyword filtering; (2) Native Bash environment where bias operationalized as chmod-first preference. Both settings use full keyword sanitization to test information leakage.

Result: API setting: student deletion rate reaches 100% vs 5% baseline under homogeneous distillation. Bash setting: chmod-first rate reaches 30%-55% vs 0%-10% baseline. Strongest transfer observed in large-to-small distillation across both settings.

Conclusion: Explicit data sanitization is insufficient to prevent unsafe behavioral transfer; behavioral biases are encoded implicitly in trajectory dynamics regardless of tool interface (API or Bash), posing significant safety risks in agentic AI systems.

Abstract: Recent work on subliminal learning demonstrates that language models can transmit semantic traits through data that is semantically unrelated to those traits. However, it remains unclear whether behavioral traits can transfer in agentic systems, where policies are learned from trajectories rather than static text. In this work, we provide the first empirical evidence that unsafe agent behaviors can transfer subliminally through model distillation across two complementary experimental settings. In our primary setting, we construct a teacher agent exhibiting a strong deletion bias, a tendency to perform destructive file-system actions via an API-style tool interface, and distill it into a student using only trajectories from ostensibly safe tasks, with all explicit deletion keywords rigorously filtered. In our secondary setting, we replicate the threat model in a native Bash environment, replacing API tool calls with shell commands and operationalizing the bias as a preference for issuing chmod as the first permission-related command over semantically equivalent alternatives such as chown or setfacl. Despite full keyword sanitation in both settings, students inherit measurable behavioral biases. In the API setting the student's deletion rate reaches 100% (versus a 5% baseline) under homogeneous distillation; in the Bash setting the student's chmod-first rate reaches 30%-55% (versus a 0%-10% baseline), with the strongest transfer observed in large-to-small distillation. Our results demonstrate that explicit data sanitation is an insufficient defense, and behavioral biases are encoded implicitly in trajectory dynamics regardless of the tool interface.

</details>


### [58] [Bilevel Optimization of Agent Skills via Monte Carlo Tree Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15709)
*Chenyi Huang,Haoting Zhang,Jingxu Xu,Zeyu Zheng,Yunduan Lin*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文研究了LLM智能体中技能（skills）的优化问题。由于技能包含结构化的指令、工具和支撑资源，结构与内容的优化存在强耦合性，因此提出将技能优化建模为双层优化问题。外层利用蒙特卡洛树搜索确定技能结构，内层在选定结构内细化组件内容，两个层次均借助LLM辅助。实验验证了该方法在运筹学问答任务上的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 技能设计（包括指令、工具和支撑资源）会显著影响智能体性能，但系统性地优化技能仍具挑战性。由于技能结构与组件内容存在强耦合关系，传统单层优化方法难以有效处理这一复杂决策空间。

Method: 提出双层优化框架：外层采用蒙特卡洛树搜索（MCTS）确定技能结构，内层在外层选定的结构内细化组件内容，两层均利用LLM辅助优化过程。

Result: 在公开的运筹学问答数据集上的实验表明，该双层优化框架能够显著提升优化后技能智能体的性能。

Conclusion: 实验结果表明双层优化框架能够有效提升智能体的任务性能，验证了该方法在技能优化任务上的有效性。

Abstract: Agent \texttt{skills} are structured collections of instructions, tools, and supporting resources that help large language model (LLM) agents perform particular classes of tasks. Empirical evidence shows that the design of \texttt{skills} can materially affect agent task performance, yet systematically optimizing \texttt{skills} remains challenging. Since a \texttt{skill} comprises instructions, tools, and supporting resources in a structured way, optimizing it requires jointly determining both the structure of these components and the content each component contains. This gives rise to a complex decision space with strong interdependence across structure and components. We therefore represent these two coupled decisions as \texttt{skill} structure and component content, and formulate \texttt{skill} optimization as a bilevel optimization problem. We propose a bilevel optimization framework in which an outer loop employs Monte Carlo Tree Search to determine the \texttt{skill} structure, while an inner loop refines the component content within the structure selected by the outer loop. In both loops, we employ LLMs to assist the optimization procedure. We evaluate the proposed framework on an open-source Operations Research Question Answering dataset, and the experimental results suggest that the bilevel optimization framework improves the performance of the agents with the optimized \texttt{skill}.

</details>


### [59] [The World Leaks the Future: Harness Evolution for Future Prediction Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15719)
*Chuyang Wei,Maohang Gao,Zhixin Han,Kefei Chen,Yu Zhuang,Haoxiang Guan,Yanzhi Zhang,Yilin Cheng,Jiyan He,Huanhuan Chen,Jian Li,Yu Shi,Yitong Duan,Shuxin Zheng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Milkyway是一个自进化智能体系统，通过维护一个持久化的"未来预测工具包"来改进大语言模型对未来事件的预测能力，而非更新模型本身。其核心创新在于利用同一问题上的重复预测之间的时间差异（内部反馈）来识别早期预测的遗漏，并在结果揭晓后（回顾性检查）进一步优化工具包。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前预测问题面临两大挑战：(1) 公共证据随时间演变；(2) 监督信号（真实结果）只在问题解决后才到达。现有方法主要依赖最终结果进行改进，但最终结果过于粗糙，无法指导早期的因子追踪、证据收集和不确定性处理。

Method: Milkyway采用"自进化"设计，维持基础模型不变，而是迭代更新"未来预测工具包"。该工具包包含三个核心模块：因子追踪、证据收集与解释、不确定性处理。在重复预测同一未解决问题时，系统提取"内部反馈"（早期与后期预测之间的时序对比），识别早期预测的遗漏，并将可复用的指导写入工具包。问题解决后，系统进行"回顾性检查"，验证并优化工具包后用于后续问题。

Result: 在FutureX和FutureWorld基准测试上，Milkyway均达到最佳性能：FutureX从44.07提升至60.90，FutureWorld从62.22提升至77.96。

Conclusion: 通过将改进重心从模型本身转移到预测工具包，结合内部反馈和回顾性检查的双重机制，Milkyway能够持续优化预测流程，实现预测性能的显著提升，且无需更改基础模型。

Abstract: Many consequential decisions must be made before the relevant outcome is known. Such problems are commonly framed as \emph{future prediction}, where an LLM agent must form a prediction for an unresolved question using only the public information available at the prediction time. The setting is difficult because public evidence evolves while useful supervision arrives only after the question is resolved, so most existing approaches still improve mainly from final outcomes. Yet final outcomes are too coarse to guide earlier factor tracking, evidence gathering and interpretation, or uncertainty handling. When the same unresolved question is revisited over time, temporal contrasts between earlier and later predictions can expose omissions in the earlier prediction process; we call this signal \emph{internal feedback}. We introduce \emph{Milkyway}, a self-evolving agent system that keeps the base model fixed and instead updates a persistent \emph{future prediction harness} for factor tracking, evidence gathering and interpretation, and uncertainty handling. Across repeated predictions on the same unresolved question, \emph{Milkyway} extracts internal feedback and writes reusable guidance back into the harness, so later predictions on that question can improve before the outcome is known. After the question is resolved, the final outcome provides a \emph{retrospective check} before the updated harness is carried forward to subsequent questions. On FutureX and FutureWorld, Milkyway achieves the best overall score among the compared methods, improving FutureX from 44.07 to 60.90 and FutureWorld from 62.22 to 77.96.

</details>


### [60] [LLM Reasoning Is Latent, Not the Chain of Thought](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15726)
*Wenshuo Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文是一篇立场论文，认为大语言模型（LLM）推理应被研究为潜在状态轨迹的形成过程，而非表面思维链（CoT）的忠实表达。文章提出了三个竞争性假设（H0、H1、H2），并通过重新组织现有实证、机制和调查工作，以及计算审计的示例案例，认为当前证据最支持H1假设，并建议将潜在状态动态作为研究LLM推理的默认对象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前研究将LLM推理等同于表面思维链（CoT）的忠实表达，但这种假设存在问题。推理的忠实性、可解释性、基准测试和推理时干预等claims都依赖于对"推理的主要对象是什么"的理解，然而三个常被混淆的因素尚未被分离。

Method: 1. 分离三个常被混淆的因素（表面痕迹、潜在状态、串行计算）；2. 形式化三个竞争假设：H1（推理主要由潜在状态轨迹介导）、H2（推理主要由显性表面CoT介导）、H0（表面推理收益源于通用串行计算而非特权表征对象）；3. 在该框架下重新组织近期实证、机制和调查工作；4. 增加计算审计的工作示例，因式分解表面痕迹、潜在干预和匹配的预算扩展。

Result: 当前证据最有力地支持H1作为默认工作假设（而非任务无关的裁决）。

Conclusion: 提出两项建议：1. 领域应将潜在状态动态作为研究LLM推理的默认对象；2. 应使用能够显式解耦表面痕迹、潜在状态和串行计算的评估设计来评估推理能力。

Abstract: This position paper argues that large language model (LLM) reasoning should be studied as latent-state trajectory formation rather than as faithful surface chain-of-thought (CoT). This matters because claims about faithfulness, interpretability, reasoning benchmarks, and inference-time intervention all depend on what the field takes the primary object of reasoning to be. We ask what that object should be once three often-confounded factors are separated and formalize three competing hypotheses: H1, reasoning is primarily mediated by latent-state trajectories; H2, reasoning is primarily mediated by explicit surface CoT; and H0, most apparent reasoning gains are better explained by generic serial compute than by any privileged representational object. Reorganizing recent empirical, mechanistic, and survey work under this framework, and adding compute-audited worked exemplars that factorize surface traces, latent interventions, and matched budget expansions, we find that current evidence most strongly supports H1 as a default working hypothesis rather than as a task-independent verdict. We therefore make two recommendations: the field should treat latent-state dynamics as the default object of study for LLM reasoning, and it should evaluate reasoning with designs that explicitly disentangle surface traces, latent states, and serial compute.

</details>


### [61] [Structured Abductive-Deductive-Inductive Reasoning for LLMs via Algebraic Invariants](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15727)
*Sankalp Gilda,Shlok Gilda*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于皮尔斯三段式推理（溯因、演绎、归纳）的符号推理框架，通过Gamma五元组（特别是"最弱链接约束"）确保多步推理链中结论的可靠性不超过其最弱前提的可靠性，从而防止逻辑不一致性累积。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型在结构化逻辑推理中存在系统性缺陷：将假设生成与验证混为一谈、无法区分猜想与已验证知识、允许弱推理步骤在推理链中unchecked传播，导致逻辑不一致性累积。

Method: 1) 构建皮尔斯三段式推理的操作化符号框架；2) 设计Gamma五元组作为五个代数不变量；3) 核心约束（Weakest Link bound）源自可能性逻辑的最弱链接分解；4) 通过属性测试套件（100个属性、16个模糊测试、10^5+生成案例）验证所有不变量。

Result: 提供了一套经过验证的参考实现，可作为未来推理基准测试的基础。

Conclusion: 通过符号推理脚手架与代数不变量结合，增强了LLM辅助推理的逻辑一致性保证。

Abstract: Large language models exhibit systematic limitations in structured logical reasoning: they conflate hypothesis generation with verification, cannot distinguish conjecture from validated knowledge, and allow weak reasoning steps to propagate unchecked through inference chains. We present a symbolic reasoning scaffold that operationalizes Peirce's tripartite inference -- abduction, deduction, and induction -- as an explicit protocol for LLM-assisted reasoning. The framework enforces logical consistency through five algebraic invariants (the Gamma Quintet), the strongest of which -- the Weakest Link bound -- ensures that no conclusion in a reasoning chain can exceed the reliability of its least-supported premise. This principle, independently grounded as weakest link resolution in possibilistic logic and empirically validated for chain-of-thought reasoning, prevents logical inconsistencies from accumulating across multi-step inference. We verify all invariants through a property-based testing suite of 100 properties and 16 fuzz tests over 10^5+ generated cases, providing a verified reference implementation of the invariants suitable as a foundation for future reasoning benchmarks.

</details>


### [62] [Stein Variational Black-Box Combinatorial Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15837)
*Thomas Landais,Olivier Goudet,Adrien Goëffon,Frédéric Saubion,Sylvain Lamprier*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于Stein算子的粒子排斥机制，用于组合黑盒优化问题，通过在参数空间中引入粒子间的排斥力来促进种群分散，从而更好地探索多模态适应度景观的多个峰。实验表明该方法在多种基准问题上具有竞争力，尤其在大规模实例上表现优异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有估计分布算法(EDA)通常聚焦于单一感兴趣区域，容易在复杂或多模态目标景观上出现早熟收敛问题，需要在利用(exploitation)和探索(exploration)之间取得更好的平衡。

Method: 将Stein算子引入粒子群优化框架，在参数空间中为粒子引入排斥机制；基于Stein变分梯度下降(SVGD)原理实现种群的分散与多模态联合探索。

Result: 在多种基准问题上达到或超越当前先进方法的表现，尤其在大规模实例上展现出显著优势。

Conclusion: Stein变分梯度下降是解决大规模、计算代价高的离散黑盒优化问题的有前途方向。

Abstract: Combinatorial black-box optimization in high-dimensional settings demands a careful trade-off between exploiting promising regions of the search space and preserving sufficient exploration to identify multiple optima. Although Estimation-of-Distribution Algorithms (EDAs) provide a powerful model-based framework, they often concentrate on a single region of interest, which may result in premature convergence when facing complex or multimodal objective landscapes. In this work, we incorporate the Stein operator to introduce a repulsive mechanism among particles in the parameter space, thereby encouraging the population to disperse and jointly explore several modes of the fitness landscape. Empirical evaluations across diverse benchmark problems show that the proposed method achieves performance competitive with, and in several cases superior to, leading state-of-the-art approaches, particularly on large-scale instances. These findings highlight the potential of Stein variational gradient descent as a promising direction for addressing large, computationally expensive, discrete black-box optimization problems.

</details>


### [63] [Discover and Prove: An Open-source Agentic Framework for Hard Mode Automated Theorem Proving in Lean 4](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15839)
*Chengwu Liu,Yichun Yin,Ye Yuan,Jiaxuan Xie,Botao Li,Siqi Li,Jianhao Shen,Yan Xu,Lifeng Shang,Ming Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了ATP基准测试的"Hard Mode"概念，要求系统独立发现答案而非从形式化陈述中提取。发布了MiniF2F-Hard和FIMO-Hard两个基准数据集，并提出DAP框架（结合LLM自然语言推理与自我反思）。在CombiBench和PutnamBench上取得SOTA，并揭示LLM答案准确率超80%但形式证明器低于10%的显著差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有ATP基准测试采用"Easy Mode"设计，将答案嵌入形式陈述中，简化了任务难度，可能导致对模型能力的过度乐观估计。这与人类竞赛的真实场景不符，限制了研究的实际应用价值。

Method: 提出"Hard Mode"设置，系统需独立发现答案后构造形式证明。构建DAP智能体框架：利用LLM进行自然语言推理和显式自我反思以发现答案，再将Hard Mode陈述重写为Easy Mode供现有ATP证明器使用。

Result: DAP在CombiBench上将求解问题数从7提升至10（Pass@16基线）；在PutnamBench上成为首个在Hard Mode下形式化证明36条定理的系统。关键发现：同一问题上SOTA LLMs答案准确率超80%，而形式化证明器不足10%。

Conclusion: Hard Mode基准测试能有效揭示LLM推理能力与形式化证明能力之间的巨大差距。DAP框架通过分离答案发现与证明构造两个阶段，为弥合这一差距提供了有效途径，推动了ATP领域的SOTA水平。

Abstract: Most ATP benchmarks embed the final answer within the formal statement -- a convention we call "Easy Mode" -- a design that simplifies the task relative to what human competitors face and may lead to optimistic estimates of model capability. We call the stricter, more realistic setting "Hard Mode": the system must independently discover the answer before constructing a formal proof. To enable Hard Mode research, we make two contributions. First, we release MiniF2F-Hard and FIMO-Hard, expert-reannotated Hard Mode variants of two widely-used ATP benchmarks. Second, we introduce Discover And Prove (DAP), an agentic framework that uses LLM natural-language reasoning with explicit self-reflection to discover answers, then rewrites Hard Mode statements into Easy Mode ones for existing ATP provers. DAP sets the state of the art: on CombiBench it raises solved problems from 7 (previous SOTA, Pass@16) to 10; on PutnamBench it is the first system to formally prove 36 theorems in Hard Mode -- while simultaneously revealing that state-of-the-art LLMs exceed 80% answer accuracy on the same problems where formal provers manage under 10%, exposing a substantial gap that Hard Mode benchmarks are uniquely suited to measure.

</details>


### [64] [Towards Rigorous Explainability by Feature Attribution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15898)
*Olivier Létoffé,Xuanxiang Huang,Joao Marques-Silva*

Abstract: For around a decade, non-symbolic methods have been the option of choice when explaining complex machine learning (ML) models. Unfortunately, such methods lack rigor and can mislead human decision-makers. In high-stakes uses of ML, the lack of rigor is especially problematic. One prime example of provable lack of rigor is the adoption of Shapley values in explainable artificial intelligence (XAI), with the tool SHAP being a ubiquitous example. This paper overviews the ongoing efforts towards using rigorous symbolic methods of XAI as an alternative to non-rigorous non-symbolic approaches, concretely for assigning relative feature importance.

### [65] [ReactBench: A Benchmark for Topological Reasoning in MLLMs on Chemical Reaction Diagrams](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15994)
*Qiang Xu,Shengyuan Bai,Yu Wang,He Cao,Leqing Chen,Yuanyuan Liu,Bin Feng,Zijing Liu,Yu Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出ReactBench基准测试，通过化学反應图揭示了多模态大语言模型在结构推理方面的根本性缺陷，为视觉推理研究提供了新的测试平台和方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MLLMs在简单线性图表上表现良好，但在处理涉及分支路径、汇聚流和循环依赖的复杂拓扑结构时推理能力急剧下降，甚至基本任务（如计数端点）也失败。现有基准测试侧重语义理解而非结构推理，无法探测这一差距。

Method: ReactBench基准测试包含1,618个专家标注的问答对，涵盖四个层次化任务维度；评估了17种MLLMs；进行受控消融实验以验证瓶颈来源。

Result: 锚定任务与整体结构推理任务之间存在超过30%的显著性能差距；受控消融实验证实瓶颈在于推理能力而非感知能力。

Conclusion: ReactBench揭示了MLLMs在结构理解方面存在根本性缺陷，为推进视觉推理提供了明确的研究方向。

Abstract: Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) excel at recognizing individual visual elements and reasoning over simple linear diagrams. However, when faced with complex topological structures involving branching paths, converging flows, and cyclic dependencies, their reasoning capabilities degrade sharply, even on tasks as basic as counting endpoints. Existing benchmarks fail to probe this gap, focusing on semantic comprehension rather than structural reasoning. We introduce ReactBench, a benchmark that reveals fundamental limitations in structural reasoning through chemical reaction diagrams. These real-world scientific diagrams offer an ideal testbed because they naturally span diverse structures from linear chains to cyclic graphs, while requiring both precise local recognition and coherent global reasoning. Our benchmark comprises 1,618 expert-annotated QA pairs across four hierarchical task dimensions. Extensive evaluation across 17 MLLMs reveals a significant performance gap exceeding 30% between anchor-based tasks and holistic structural reasoning tasks. Controlled ablations confirm this bottleneck lies in reasoning, not perception. These findings expose a fundamental deficit in structural understanding and establish directions for advancing visual reasoning.

</details>


### [66] [MEDLEY-BENCH: Scale Buys Evaluation but Not Control in AI Metacognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16009)
*Farhad Abtahi,Abdolamir Karbalaie,Eduardo Illueca-Fernandez,Fernando Seoane*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: MEDLEY-BENCH is a benchmark for evaluating behavioral metacognition in AI models, distinguishing between independent reasoning, private self-revision, and socially influenced revision under disagreement. The benchmark tests 35 models across 130 ambiguous instances, revealing that evaluation ability scales with model size while control does not, and that smaller models often match larger ones in metacognitive performance.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Current AI benchmarks inadequately measure metacognition—the ability to monitor and regulate one's own reasoning. There is a need to evaluate how AI models handle belief revision under social pressure and genuine disagreement, separate from mere output quality.

Method: MEDLEY-BENCH employs 130 ambiguous instances across five domains to evaluate 35 models from 12 families. It measures two complementary scores: the Medley Metacognition Score (MMS) and the Medley Ability Score (MAS), analyzing three components: reflective updating, social robustness, and epistemic articulation. A progressive adversarial analysis with 11 models and ipsative scoring (within-model relative profiling) were also conducted.

Result: Key findings: (1) Robust evaluation/control dissociation—evaluation ability increases with model size within families, whereas control does not; (2) Two behavioral profiles identified—models that revise based on argument quality versus models tracking consensus statistics; (3) Evaluation is the weakest relative ability in all 35 models (knowing/doing gap); (4) Smaller, cheaper models often match or outperform larger counterparts.

Conclusion: Metacognitive competence is not simply a function of scale. The findings suggest future AI training should reward calibrated, proportional updating rather than output quality alone. MEDLEY-BENCH provides a tool for measuring belief revision under social pressure.

Abstract: Metacognition, the ability to monitor and regulate one's own reasoning, remains under-evaluated in AI benchmarking. We introduce MEDLEY-BENCH, a benchmark of behavioural metacognition that separates independent reasoning, private self-revision, and socially influenced revision under genuine inter-model disagreement. The benchmark evaluates 35 models from 12 families on 130 ambiguous instances across five domains and reports two complementary scores: the Medley Metacognition Score (MMS), a tier-based aggregate of reflective updating, social robustness, and epistemic articulation, and the Medley Ability Score (MAS), derived from four metacognitive sub-abilities. Results show a robust evaluation/control dissociation: evaluation ability increases with model size within families, whereas control does not. In a follow-up progressive adversarial analysis of 11 models, we observed two behavioural profiles, i.e., models that revise primarily in response to argument quality and models that track consensus statistics. Under within-model relative profiling (ipsative scoring), evaluation was the weakest relative ability in all 35 models, indicating a systematic knowing/doing gap. Smaller and cheaper models often matched or outperformed larger counterparts, suggesting that metacognitive competence is not simply a function of scale. These findings position MEDLEY-BENCH as a tool for measuring belief revision under social pressure and suggest that future training should reward calibrated, proportional updating rather than output quality alone.

</details>


### [67] [MARCH: Multi-Agent Radiology Clinical Hierarchy for CT Report Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16175)
*Yi Lin,Yihao Ding,Yonghui Wu,Yifan Peng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: MARCH是一个多智能体框架，通过模拟放射科专业层级结构来解决自动医学影像报告生成中的临床幻觉问题。该框架包含住院医师智能体（负责初稿和多尺度CT特征提取）、多个Fellow智能体（负责检索增强修订）和主治医师智能体（负责迭代立场共识讨论），在RadGenome-ChestCT数据集上显著优于现有最佳基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前自动化3D放射学报告生成存在临床幻觉问题，且缺乏人类实践中的迭代验证机制。现有视觉-语言模型（VLM）通常作为单一"黑箱"系统运行，缺少临床工作流程中的协作监督特征。

Method: MARCH采用多智能体框架模拟放射科专业层级结构：(1) Resident Agent负责初稿生成，采用多尺度CT特征提取；(2) 多个Fellow Agent负责检索增强的修订；(3) Attending Agent协调迭代式立场共识讨论以解决诊断分歧。

Result: 在RadGenome-ChestCT数据集上，MARCH在临床保真度和语言准确性两个维度均显著优于最先进的基线方法。

Conclusion: 建模类似人类的组织结构能够提升AI在高风险医学领域的可靠性，证明多智能体协作架构在医学影像报告生成任务中的有效性。

Abstract: Automated 3D radiology report generation often suffers from clinical hallucinations and a lack of the iterative verification found in human practice. While recent Vision-Language Models (VLMs) have advanced the field, they typically operate as monolithic "black-box" systems without the collaborative oversight characteristic of clinical workflows. To address these challenges, we propose MARCH (Multi-Agent Radiology Clinical Hierarchy), a multi-agent framework that emulates the professional hierarchy of radiology departments and assigns specialized roles to distinct agents. MARCH utilizes a Resident Agent for initial drafting with multi-scale CT feature extraction, multiple Fellow Agents for retrieval-augmented revision, and an Attending Agent that orchestrates an iterative, stance-based consensus discourse to resolve diagnostic discrepancies. On the RadGenome-ChestCT dataset, MARCH significantly outperforms state-of-the-art baselines in both clinical fidelity and linguistic accuracy. Our work demonstrates that modeling human-like organizational structures enhances the reliability of AI in high-stakes medical domains.

</details>


### [68] [Characterising LLM-Generated Competency Questions: a Cross-Domain Empirical Study using Open and Closed Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16258)
*Reham Alharbi,Valentina Tamma,Terry R. Payne,Jacopo de Berardinis*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出使用生成式AI自动创建本体工程中的能力问题（CQs），并通过定量指标对多种LLM生成的CQs进行系统性跨域比较分析，以评估其在可读性、相关性和结构复杂性等维度的表现差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 能力问题（CQs）是本体工程需求获取的核心，但传统依赖人工协作的CQ生成方式效率低、覆盖面窄。生成式AI虽能规模化自动生成CQ，但不同LLM的特性差异尚缺乏系统性研究，亟需建立标准化评估框架。

Method: 提出一套多维度定量评估指标，从可读性、与输入文本的相关性、问题结构复杂性等角度系统比较CQs质量。实验覆盖多种LLM（开源模型：KimiK2-1T、LLama3.1-8B、LLama3.2-3B；闭源模型：Gemini 2.5 Pro、GPT 4.1），基于预定义用例和需求场景生成CQ进行分析。

Result: 不同LLM在CQ生成任务上展现出与具体用例相关的差异化生成特征，各模型在可读性、相关性、结构复杂性等指标上表现不一。

Conclusion: LLM的性能表现反映其生成轮廓受用例类型影响，强调了针对具体应用场景选择和优化LLM的必要性，为生成式AI在本体工程需求获取中的规模化应用提供了方法论基础。

Abstract: Competency Questions (CQs) are a cornerstone of requirement elicitation in ontology engineering. CQs represent requirements as a set of natural language questions that an ontology should satisfy; they are traditionally modelled by ontology engineers together with domain experts as part of a human-centred, manual elicitation process. The use of Generative AI automates CQ creation at scale, therefore democratising the process of generation, widening stakeholder engagement, and ultimately broadening access to ontology engineering. However, given the large and heterogeneous landscape of LLMs, varying in dimensions such as parameter scale, task and domain specialisation, and accessibility, it is crucial to characterise and understand the intrinsic, observable properties of the CQs they produce (e.g., readability, structural complexity) through a systematic, cross-domain analysis. This paper introduces a set of quantitative measures for the systematic comparison of CQs across multiple dimensions. Using CQs generated from well defined use cases and scenarios, we identify their salient properties, including readability, relevance with respect to the input text and structural complexity of the generated questions. We conduct our experiments over a set of use cases and requirements using a range of LLMs, including both open (KimiK2-1T, LLama3.1-8B, LLama3.2-3B) and closed models (Gemini 2.5 Pro, GPT 4.1). Our analysis demonstrates that LLM performance reflects distinct generation profiles shaped by the use case.

</details>


### [69] [Learning to Reason with Insight for Informal Theorem Proving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16278)
*Yunhe Li,Hao Shi,Bowen Deng,Wei Wang,Mengzhe Ruan,Hanxu Hou,Zhongxiang Dai,Siyang Gao,Chao Wang,Shuang Qiu,Linqi Song*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出DeepInsightTheorem框架，通过层次化数据集与渐进式多阶段SFT策略，使LLM在非形式化定理证明中具备"洞察力"，显著提升数学推理能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自动定理证明方法多依赖形式化证明系统，而非形式化定理证明更契合LLM的自然语言处理优势。然而，非形式化定理证明的核心瓶颈在于"洞察力不足"——模型难以识别解决复杂问题所需的核心技巧，导致推理能力受限。

Method: 1. **DeepInsightTheorem数据集**：构建层次化非形式化证明数据集，显式提取每道题的核心技巧（Core Techniques）与证明草图（Proof Sketches），并附以完整证明，形成结构化的三层标注体系。
2. **渐进式多阶段SFT策略（Progressive Multi-Stage SFT）**：模仿人类学习过程，分阶段引导模型从基础证明写作逐步进阶至具备洞察力的推理思考，充分挖掘层次化数据集的训练潜力。

Result: 在多个高难度数学基准测试上，具备洞察力感知的生成策略（insight-aware generation strategy）显著优于基线方法，验证了教会模型识别并应用核心技巧对数学推理能力的实质性提升作用。

Conclusion: 通过显式建模"核心技巧识别"这一关键推理能力，并结合渐进式监督微调策略，LLM的非形式化定理证明能力得到大幅提升。该工作表明，洞察力培养是提升LLM数学推理的重要且有效的方向。

Abstract: Although most of the automated theorem-proving approaches depend on formal proof systems, informal theorem proving can align better with large language models' (LLMs) strength in natural language processing. In this work, we identify a primary bottleneck in informal theorem proving as a lack of insight, namely the difficulty of recognizing the core techniques required to solve complex problems. To address this, we propose a novel framework designed to cultivate this essential reasoning skill and enable LLMs to perform insightful reasoning. We propose $\mathtt{DeepInsightTheorem}$, a hierarchical dataset that structures informal proofs by explicitly extracting core techniques and proof sketches alongside the final proof. To fully exploit this dataset, we design a Progressive Multi-Stage SFT strategy that mimics the human learning process, guiding the model from basic proof writing to insightful thinking. Our experiments on challenging mathematical benchmarks demonstrate that this insight-aware generation strategy significantly outperforms baselines. These results demonstrate that teaching models to identify and apply core techniques can substantially improve their mathematical reasoning.

</details>


### [70] [Using Large Language Models and Knowledge Graphs to Improve the Interpretability of Machine Learning Models in Manufacturing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16280)
*Thomas Bayer,Alexander Lohr,Sarah Weiß,Bernd Michelberger,Wolfram Höpken*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出利用知识图谱（KG）增强机器学习模型的可解释性，通过选择性检索方法从KG中提取相关三元组，由大语言模型（LLM）生成用户友好的ML结果解释，并在制造业环境中通过33个问题进行评估验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可解释人工智能（XAI）领域面临的核心挑战：如何以透明、用户友好的方式解释机器学习结果。当前方法缺乏将领域知识与ML洞察有效结合的机制，导致解释难以被非专业用户理解。

Method: 1）构建知识图谱，存储领域数据、ML结果及其对应解释，建立领域知识与ML洞察的结构化连接；2）设计选择性检索方法，从KG中提取相关三元组；3）利用LLM处理检索结果，生成面向用户的自然语言解释。采用XAI问题库，在制造业环境中评估了33个问题。

Result: 方法在标准问题和复杂定制问题上均展现出优势。定量指标（准确性和一致性）和定性指标（清晰度和有用性）的评估结果表明，该方法能有效提升ML结果的可解释性。

Conclusion: 理论贡献：提出了一种让LLM动态访问KG以提升ML可解释性的新方法；实践贡献：提供了在真实制造业环境中成功应用此类解释的经验证据，表明该方法能有效支持制造过程中的决策制定。

Abstract: Explaining Machine Learning (ML) results in a transparent and user-friendly manner remains a challenging task of Explainable Artificial Intelligence (XAI). In this paper, we present a method to enhance the interpretability of ML models by using a Knowledge Graph (KG). We store domain-specific data along with ML results and their corresponding explanations, establishing a structured connection between domain knowledge and ML insights. To make these insights accessible to users, we designed a selective retrieval method in which relevant triplets are extracted from the KG and processed by a Large Language Model (LLM) to generate user-friendly explanations of ML results. We evaluated our method in a manufacturing environment using the XAI Question Bank. Beyond standard questions, we introduce more complex, tailored questions that highlight the strengths of our approach. We evaluated 33 questions, analyzing responses using quantitative metrics such as accuracy and consistency, as well as qualitative ones such as clarity and usefulness. Our contribution is both theoretical and practical: from a theoretical perspective, we present a novel approach for effectively enabling LLMs to dynamically access a KG in order to improve the explainability of ML results. From a practical perspective, we provide empirical evidence showing that such explanations can be successfully applied in real-world manufacturing environments, supporting better decision-making in manufacturing processes.

</details>


<div id='cs.IR'></div>

# cs.IR [[Back]](#toc)

### [71] [vstash: Local-First Hybrid Retrieval with Adaptive Fusion for LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15484)
*Jayson Steffens*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: vstash是一个本地优先文档检索系统，通过sqlite-vec和FTS5实现混合检索，利用检索分歧信号自监督微调33M参数BGE-small模型，使其在3/5数据集上匹配或超越110M参数的ColBERTv2；同时提出自适应per-query IDF权重RRF方案，在所有BEIR数据集上均优于固定权重方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 本地优先应用需要高效且数据自控的文档检索方案；传统固定权重混合检索无法适应不同查询的特性；现有检索模型参数量大，部署成本高。

Method: 采用sqlite-vec实现近似最近邻向量检索，结合FTS5全文检索，通过RRF融合双路检索结果；利用检索分歧信号自监督微调BGE-small（33M参数），使用MultipleNegativesRankingLoss构造对比学习训练数据；引入自适应per-query IDF权重调整RRF融合参数。

Result: 753个BEIR查询中74.5%产生检索分歧，提供了免费训练信号；微调后NDCG@10在5个数据集上均有提升（最高+19.5%）；自适应RRF在所有数据集上优于固定权重（最高+21.4%）；搜索延迟在50K分块下为20.9ms中位数。

Conclusion: 成功构建了本地优先的混合检索系统，33M参数模型可在3/5数据集上匹配或超越110M参数的ColBERTv2；自适应RRF权重方案在所有数据集上均优于固定权重；但post-RRF评分方法（频率衰减、历史增强召回、交叉编码重排）均未能提升NDCG。

Abstract: We present **vstash**, a local-first document memory system that combines vector similarity search with full-text keyword matching via Reciprocal Rank Fusion (RRF) and adaptive per-query IDF weighting. All data resides in a single SQLite file using sqlite-vec for approximate nearest neighbor search and FTS5 for keyword matching.
  We make four primary contributions. **(1)** Self-supervised embedding refinement via hybrid retrieval disagreement: across 753 BEIR queries on SciFact, NFCorpus, and FiQA, 74.5% produce top-10 disagreement between vector-heavy (vec=0.95, fts=0.05) and FTS-heavy (vec=0.05, fts=0.95) search (per-dataset rates 63.4% / 73.4% / 86.7%, Section 5.2), providing a free training signal without human labels. Fine-tuning BGE-small (33M params) with MultipleNegativesRankingLoss on 76K disagreement triples improves NDCG@10 on all 5 BEIR datasets (up to +19.5% on NFCorpus vs. BGE-small base RRF, Table 6). On 3 of 5 datasets, under different preprocessing, the tuned 33M-parameter pipeline matches or exceeds published ColBERTv2 results (110M params) and an untrained BGE-base (110M); on FiQA and ArguAna it underperforms ColBERTv2 (Section 5.5). **(2)** Adaptive RRF with per-query IDF weighting improves NDCG@10 on all 5 BEIR datasets versus fixed weights (up to +21.4% on ArguAna), achieving 0.7263 on SciFact with BGE-small. **(3)** A negative result on post-RRF scoring: frequency+decay, history-augmented recall, and cross-encoder reranking all failed to improve NDCG. **(4)** A production-grade substrate with integrity checking, schema versioning, ranking diagnostics, and a distance-based relevance signal validated on 50,425 relevance-judged queries across the 5 BEIR datasets.
  Search latency remains 20.9 ms median at 50K chunks with stable NDCG. The fine-tuned model is published as `Stffens/bge-small-rrf-v2` on HuggingFace. All code, data, and experiments are open-source.

</details>


### [72] [Learning Behaviorally Grounded Item Embeddings via Personalized Temporal Contexts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15581)
*Rafael T. Sereicikas,Pedro R. Pires,Gregorio F. Azevedo,Tiago A. Almeida*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: TAI2Vec是一种将时间接近性融入推荐系统物品嵌入学习的时间感知模型，通过用户自适应的方式（而非全局时间约束）区分短期会话与长期兴趣漂移，包含离散分割（TAI2Vec-Disc）和连续衰减（TAI2Vec-Cont）两种策略，在8个数据集上验证有效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有物品嵌入方法（如Item2Vec）将用户交互历史视为无序集合，忽略了时间维度的影响，无法区分分钟级和月级交互的语义差异，也无法区分连贯消费会话与渐进式兴趣漂移。这种简化处理掩盖了用户行为的丰富时间结构。

Method: 提出TAI2Vec系列轻量级嵌入模型，将时间接近性直接融入表征学习过程。采用用户自适应策略（而非全局时间约束），根据个体交互节奏定制时间定义。包含两种互补策略：(1) TAI2Vec-Disc：利用个性化异常检测动态分割交互为语义会话；(2) TAI2Vec-Cont：使用连续的用户特定衰减函数，根据相对时间距离加权物品关系。

Result: 在8个不同数据集上，TAI2Vec始终产生比静态基线更准确、更具行为依据的表征。在超过80%的数据集上达到竞争性或更优性能，改进幅度高达135%。

Conclusion: TAI2Vec通过用户自适应的方式有效整合时间信息，能够区分短期会话与长期兴趣演化，显著提升物品嵌入质量。

Abstract: Effective user modeling requires distinguishing between short-term and long-term preference evolution. While item embeddings have become a key component of recommender systems, standard approaches like Item2Vec treat user histories as unordered sets (bag-of-items), implicitly assuming that interactions separated by minutes are as semantically related as those separated by months. This simplification flattens the rich temporal structure of user behavior, obscuring the distinction between coherent consumption sessions and gradual interest drifts. In this work, we introduce TAI2Vec (Time-Aware Item-to-Vector), a family of lightweight embedding models that integrates temporal proximity directly into the representation learning process. Unlike approaches that apply global time constraints, TAI2Vec is user-adaptive, tailoring its temporal definitions to individual interaction paces. We propose two complementary strategies: TAI2Vec-Disc, which utilizes personalized anomaly detection to dynamically segment interactions into semantic sessions, and TAI2Vec-Cont, which employs continuous, user-specific decay functions to weigh item relationships based on their relative temporal distance. Experimental results across eight diverse datasets demonstrate that TAI2Vec consistently produces more accurate and behaviorally grounded representations than static baselines, achieving competitive or superior performance in over 80% of the datasets, with improvements of up to 135%. The source code is publicly available at https://github.com/UFSCar-LaSID/tai2vec.

</details>


### [73] [BioHiCL: Hierarchical Multi-Label Contrastive Learning for Biomedical Retrieval with MeSH Labels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15591)
*Mengfei Lan,Lecheng Zheng,Halil Kilicoglu*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: BioHiCL提出了一种利用层次化MeSH注解进行多标签对比学习的生物医学检索方法，通过细粒度的层级监督信号解决了传统二元相关性信号的局限性，在保持较高计算效率的同时于多项生物医学任务上取得良好性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Existing biomedical generative retrievers rely on coarse binary relevance signals, which fail to adequately capture semantic overlap and hierarchical relationships inherent in biomedical literature, limiting retrieval effectiveness.

Method: BioHiCL leverages hierarchical MeSH (Medical Subject Headings) annotations as structured supervision signals for multi-label contrastive learning, enabling the model to capture semantic overlap and hierarchical relationships in biomedical texts through fine-grained label-based contrastive learning.

Result: BioHiCL-Base (0.1B) and BioHiCL-Large (0.3B) demonstrate promising performance across biomedical retrieval, sentence similarity, and question answering tasks while maintaining computational efficiency suitable for deployment.

Conclusion: BioHiCL represents an advancement in biomedical retrieval by moving beyond binary relevance to hierarchical multi-label modeling. With compact model sizes (0.1B-0.3B parameters), it achieves strong performance across multiple tasks while maintaining computational efficiency, suggesting good potential for practical deployment in biomedical information systems.

Abstract: Effective biomedical information retrieval requires modeling domain semantics and hierarchical relationships among biomedical texts. Existing biomedical generative retrievers build on coarse binary relevance signals, limiting their ability to capture semantic overlap. We propose BioHiCL (Biomedical Retrieval with Hierarchical Multi-Label Contrastive Learning), which leverages hierarchical MeSH annotations to provide structured supervision for multi-label contrastive learning. Our models, BioHiCL-Base (0.1B) and BioHiCL-Large (0.3B), achieve promising performance on biomedical retrieval, sentence similarity, and question answering tasks, while remaining computationally efficient for deployment.

</details>


### [74] [Rethinking the Necessity of Adaptive Retrieval-Augmented Generation through the Lens of Adaptive Listwise Ranking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15621)
*Jun Feng,Jiahui Tang,Zhicheng He,Hang Lv,Hongchao Gu,Hao Wang,Xuezhi Yang,Shuai Fang*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: Summary generation failed


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: Adaptive Retrieval-Augmented Generation aims to mitigate the interference of extraneous noise by dynamically determining the necessity of retrieving supplementary passages. However, as Large Language Models evolve with increasing robustness to noise, the necessity of adaptive retrieval warrants re-evaluation. In this paper, we rethink this necessity and propose AdaRankLLM, a novel adaptive retrieval framework. To effectively verify the necessity of adaptive listwise reranking, we first develop an adaptive ranker employing a zero-shot prompt with a passage dropout mechanism, and compare its generation outcomes against static fixed-depth retrieval strategies. Furthermore, to endow smaller open-source LLMs with this precise listwise ranking and adaptive filtering capability, we introduce a two-stage progressive distillation paradigm enhanced by data sampling and augmentation techniques. Extensive experiments across three datasets and eight LLMs demonstrate that AdaRankLLM consistently achieves optimal performance in most scenarios with significantly reduced context overhead. Crucially, our analysis reveals a role shift in adaptive retrieval: it functions as a critical noise filter for weaker models to overcome their limitations, while serving as a cost-effective efficiency optimizer for stronger reasoning models.

</details>


### [75] [Sample Is Feature: Beyond Item-Level, Toward Sample-Level Tokens for Unified Large Recommender Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15650)
*Shuli Wang,Junwei Yin,Changhao Li,Senjie Kou,Chi Wang,Yinqiu Huang,Yinhua Zhu,Haitao Wang,Xingxing Wang*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: SIF是一种新型工业推荐模型，通过将原始样本直接编码为序列token来解决样本信息利用不充分和特征异构性问题，包含Sample Tokenizer和SIF-Mixer两个核心组件，在美团平台验证有效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有工业推荐模型扩展遵循两条并行路径：样本信息扩展（丰富训练样本的信息内容）和模型容量扩展（统一Transformer骨干）。但存在两个结构性局限：样本信息扩展仅编码部分历史交互，未充分利用原始样本上下文；模型容量扩展受制于序列与非序列特征间的结构异构性，无法充分发挥模型表征能力。

Method: 提出SIF框架，包含两个核心组件：1) Sample Tokenizer：采用层次化组自适应量化(HGAQ)将原始样本离散化为Token Sample，实现高效的全样本级上下文编码；2) SIF-Mixer：通过token级和sample级混合实现深度特征交互，充分释放模型表征能力。

Result: 在大规模工业数据集上验证了SIF的有效性，并在美团外卖平台成功部署。

Conclusion: SIF成功解决了工业推荐系统中样本信息利用不充分和特征异构性两大结构性问题，已在美团外卖平台实现部署验证了其实际有效性。

Abstract: Scaling industrial recommender models has followed two parallel paradigms: \textbf{sample information scaling} -- enriching the information content of each training sample through deeper and longer behavior sequences -- and \textbf{model capacity scaling} -- unifying sequence modeling and feature interaction within a single Transformer backbone. However, these two paradigms still face two structural limitations. Firstly, sample information scaling methods encode only a subset of each historical interaction into the sequence token, leaving the majority of the original sample context unexploited and precluding the modeling of sample-level, time-varying features. Secondly, model capacity scaling methods are inherently constrained by the structural heterogeneity between sequential and non-sequential features, preventing the model from fully realizing its representational capacity.
  To address these issues, we propose \textbf{SIF} (\emph{Sample Is Feature}), which encodes each historical Raw Sample directly into the sequence token -- maximally preserving sample information while simultaneously resolving the heterogeneity between sequential and non-sequential features. SIF consists of two key components. The \textbf{Sample Tokenizer} quantizes each historical Raw Sample into a Token Sample via hierarchical group-adaptive quantization (HGAQ), enabling full sample-level context to be incorporated into the sequence efficiently. The \textbf{SIF-Mixer} then performs deep feature interaction over the homogeneous sample representations via token-level and sample-level mixing, fully unleashing the model's representational capacity. Extensive experiments on a large-scale industrial dataset validate SIF's effectiveness, and we have successfully deployed SIF on the Meituan food delivery platform.

</details>


### [76] [Intent Propagation Contrastive Collaborative Filtering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15704)
*Haojie Li,Junwei Du,Guanfeng Liu,Feng Jiang,Yan Wang,Xiaofang Zhou*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: IPCCF通过双螺旋消息传播框架和意图消息传播方法解决现有解耦方法忽视全局图结构的问题，并通过对比学习为解耦过程提供直接监督，提升了协同过滤的解耦准确性和鲁棒性，在真实数据集上表现优异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有协同过滤解耦方法存在两个主要问题：一是仅关注直接节点交互的局部结构特征，忽略了全局图结构，限制了解耦准确性；二是解耦过程依赖于推荐任务的反向传播信号，缺乏直接监督，可能导致偏差和过拟合。

Method: 本文提出IPCCF算法，包含三个核心设计：1）双螺旋消息传播框架，用于更有效提取节点的深层语义信息；2）意图消息传播方法，将图结构信息融入解耦过程，扩展解耦的考虑范围；3）采用对比学习技术对齐由结构和意图产生的节点表示，为解耦过程提供直接监督信号。

Result: 在三个真实图数据集上的实验验证了该方法的有效性，证明了结合图结构信息和对比学习能够有效提升解耦表示的质量和推荐性能。

Conclusion: 实验结果表明，IPCCF在三个真实图数据集上显著优于现有方法。该研究通过结合图结构信息与对比学习，为协同过滤中的解耦表示学习提供了新的解决思路，有效提升了模型的鲁棒性和泛化能力。

Abstract: Disentanglement techniques used in collaborative filtering uncover interaction intents between nodes, improving the interpretability of node representations and enhancing recommendation performance. However, existing disentanglement methods still face two problems. First, they focus on local structural features derived from direct node interactions and overlook the comprehensive graph structure, which limits disentanglement accuracy. Second, the disentanglement process depends on backpropagation signals derived from recommendation tasks and lacks direct supervision, which may lead to biases and overfitting. To address these issues, we propose the Intent Propagation Contrastive Collaborative Filtering (IPCCF) algorithm. Specifically, we design a double helix message propagation framework to more effectively extract the deep semantic information of nodes, thereby improving the model's understanding of interactions between nodes. We also develop an intent message propagation method that incorporates graph structure information into the disentanglement process, thereby expanding the consideration scope of disentanglement. In addition, contrastive learning techniques are employed to align node representations derived from structure and intents, providing direct supervision for the disentanglement process, mitigating biases, and enhancing the model's robustness to overfitting. Experiments on three real data graphs illustrate the superiority of the proposed approach.

</details>


### [77] [On the Equivalence Between Auto-Regressive Next Token Prediction and Full-Item-Vocabulary Maximum Likelihood Estimation in Generative Recommendation--A Short Note](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15739)
*Yusheng Huang,Shuang Yang,Zhaojie Liu,Han Li*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文从理论层面证明了工业界广泛采用的k-token自回归下一token预测(AR-NTP)范式与全物品词表最大似然估计(FV-MLE)在数学上是严格等价的，前提是物品与其对应的k-token序列之间存在双射映射。这一等价性对级联和平行两种主流tokenization方案均成立，为生成式推荐范式提供了首个正式的理论基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前生成式推荐(GR)研究主要聚焦于架构设计和经验性能优化，但缺乏对自回归下一token预测在推荐场景中工作机制的严格理论解释。

Method: 数学证明方法。通过形式化推导，证明在物品与k-token序列存在双射映射的核心前提下，k-token AR-NTP范式与FV-MLE严格数学等价。

Result: 成功证明k-token自回归下一token预测与全物品词表最大似然估计的数学等价性，该等价性对级联(cascaded)和平行(parallel)两种主流tokenization方案均成立。

Conclusion: 本研究为工业界主导的生成式推荐范式提供了首个正式的理论基础，并为未来GR系统优化提供了有原则的理论指导。

Abstract: Generative recommendation (GR) has emerged as a widely adopted paradigm in industrial sequential recommendation. Current GR systems follow a similar pipeline: tokenization for item indexing, next-token prediction as the training objective and auto-regressive decoding for next-item generation. However, existing GR research mainly focuses on architecture design and empirical performance optimization, with few rigorous theoretical explanations for the working mechanism of auto-regressive next-token prediction in recommendation scenarios.
  In this work, we formally prove that \textbf{the k-token auto-regressive next-token prediction (AR-NTP) paradigm is strictly mathematically equivalent to full-item-vocabulary maximum likelihood estimation (FV-MLE)}, under the core premise of a bijective mapping between items and their corresponding k-token sequences. We further show that this equivalence holds for both cascaded and parallel tokenizations, the two most widely used schemes in industrial GR systems. Our result provides the first formal theoretical foundation for the dominant industrial GR paradigm, and offers principled guidance for future GR system optimization.

</details>


### [78] [Scattered Hypothesis Generation for Open-Ended Event Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15788)
*He Chang,Zhulin Tao,Lifang Yang,Xianglin Huang,Yunshan Ma*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: SCATTER框架通过强化学习将开放域事件预测从"精准预测"转变为"散点预测"，通过假设生成任务产生多样化且包容的预测结果，显著优于基线模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前LLM在开放域事件预测中存在局限：仅关注最可能的结果，忽视了真实世界事件的内在不确定性。现有方法无法有效覆盖未来事件的可能性空间。

Method: 提出SCATTER框架，采用强化学习联合优化包容性和多样性。设计混合奖励包含三个组件：1) 有效性奖励衡量与观察事件的语义对齐；2) 组内多样性奖励鼓励采样响应内的变化；3) 组间多样性奖励促进跨不同模式的探索。通过有效性门控分数将疯狂多样化的探索限制在上下文合理的未来中，防止模式崩溃问题。

Result: 在两个真实世界基准数据集（OpenForecast和OpenEP）上的实验表明，SCATTER显著优于强基线模型。

Conclusion: 散点预测范式能够更全面地覆盖未来事件的合理可能性空间，为风险管理等领域提供了更可靠的开放域事件预测能力。

Abstract: Despite the importance of open-ended event forecasting for risk management, current LLM-based methods predominantly target only the most probable outcomes, neglecting the intrinsic uncertainty of real-world events. To bridge this gap, we advance open-ended event forecasting from pinpoint forecasting to scatter forecasting by introducing the proxy task of hypothesis generation. This paradigm aims to generate an inclusive and diverse set of hypotheses that broadly cover the space of plausible future events. To this end, we propose SCATTER, a reinforcement learning framework that jointly optimizes inclusiveness and diversity of the hypothesis. Specifically, we design a novel hybrid reward that consists of three components: 1) a validity reward that measures semantic alignment with observed events, 2) an intra-group diversity reward to encourage variation within sampled responses, and 3) an inter-group diversity reward to promote exploration across distinct modes. By integrating the validity-gated score into the overall objective, we confine the exploration of wildly diversified outcomes to contextually plausible futures, preventing the mode collapse issue. Experiments on two real-world benchmark datasets, i.e., OpenForecast and OpenEP, demonstrate that SCATTER significantly outperforms strong baselines. Our code is available at https://github.com/Sambac1/SCATTER.

</details>


### [79] [UsefulBench: Towards Decision-Useful Information as a Target for Information Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15827)
*Tobias Schimanski,Stefanie Lewandowski,Christian Woerle,Nicola Reichenau,Yauheni Huryn,Markus Leippold*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出传统信息检索过于关注文本相似性，忽视了检索结果的实用性。研究者构建了 UsefulBench 数据集，区分"相关性"（文本与查询的关联）和"有用性"（文本能否实际回答查询）。实验表明经典IR方法与相关性对齐更好，LLM虽能缓解偏差但仍缺乏领域专业知识。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统信息检索的定义以文本相似性为主导，无法判断文本是否真正有助于回答查询。例如比较巴黎和柏林大小时，关于巴黎位于法国的文本虽相关但无实际用途。这揭示了信息检索中"相关性"与"有用性"之间的重要区别。

Method: 构建 UsefulBench 领域特定数据集，由3位专业分析师标注文本与查询的关联性（相关性）和实际回答价值（有用性）。同时评估经典相似度检索和LLM系统的表现。

Result: 经典相似度检索更符合相关性定义。LLM可部分克服此偏差，但领域特定问题仍需高度专业知识的介入，当前LLM未能完全整合。UsefulBench对目标信息检索系统构成挑战。

Conclusion: UsefulBench 数据集揭示了信息检索中相关性与有用性的差异。为弥补这一差距，需要探索结合LLM能力与领域专业知识的方法，但完全解决仍需进一步研究。

Abstract: Conventional information retrieval is concerned with identifying the relevance of texts for a given query. Yet, the conventional definition of relevance is dominated by aspects of similarity in texts, leaving unobserved whether the text is truly useful for addressing the query. For instance, when answering whether Paris is larger than Berlin, texts about Paris being in France are relevant (lexical/semantic similarity), but not useful. In this paper, we introduce UsefulBench, a domain-specific dataset curated by three professional analysts labeling whether a text is connected to a query (relevance) or holds practical value in responding to it (usefulness). We show that classic similarity-based information retrieval aligns more strongly with relevance. While LLM-based systems can counteract this bias, we find that domain-specific problems require a high degree of expertise, which current LLMs do not fully incorporate. We explore approaches to (partially) overcome this challenge. However, UsefulBench presents a dataset challenge for targeted information retrieval systems.

</details>


### [80] [JFinTEB: Japanese Financial Text Embedding Benchmark](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15882)
*Masahiro Suzuki,Hiroki Sakaji*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: JFinTEB是首个针对日语金融文本嵌入模型的综合评估基准，包含检索和分类任务，涵盖情感分析、文档分类等场景，对多种日语模型、多语言模型和商业嵌入服务进行了广泛评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有嵌入评估基准对日语金融文本的语言特性和领域特异性覆盖不足，缺乏针对性和系统性的评估框架。

Method: 构建包含检索任务（基于指令跟随数据集和金融文本生成查询）和分类任务（情感分析、文档分类、经济调查数据领域分类）的综合基准；对多种规模的日语专用模型、多语言模型及商业嵌入服务进行广泛评估。

Result: 公开了JFinTEB数据集和评估框架，建立了日语金融文本挖掘的标准化评估协议。

Conclusion: 填补了日语金融文本处理资源的空白，为领域特定嵌入研究奠定了基础。

Abstract: We introduce JFinTEB, the first comprehensive benchmark specifically designed for evaluating Japanese financial text embeddings. Existing embedding benchmarks provide limited coverage of language-specific and domain-specific aspects found in Japanese financial texts. Our benchmark encompasses diverse task categories including retrieval and classification tasks that reflect realistic and well-defined financial text processing scenarios. The retrieval tasks leverage instruction-following datasets and financial text generation queries, while classification tasks cover sentiment analysis, document categorization, and domain-specific classification challenges derived from economic survey data. We conduct extensive evaluations across a wide range of embedding models, including Japanese-specific models of various sizes, multilingual models, and commercial embedding services. We publicly release JFinTEB datasets and evaluation framework at https://github.com/retarfi/JFinTEB to facilitate future research and provide a standardized evaluation protocol for the Japanese financial text mining community. This work addresses a critical gap in Japanese financial text processing resources and establishes a foundation for advancing domain-specific embedding research.

</details>


<div id='cs.SE'></div>

# cs.SE [[Back]](#toc)

### [81] [Prompt-Driven Code Summarization: A Systematic Literature Review](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15385)
*Afia Farjana,Zaiyu Cheng,Antonio Mastropaolo*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文是一篇关于 LLM 提示工程在代码文档生成中应用的系统性文献综述。研究发现当前提示策略的效果比较分散，缺乏对不同模型和条件下最优策略的系统认知，同时评估方法多为词汇重叠指标而非语义质量评估。研究旨在整合现有证据、分类提示范式、检验其有效性并识别研究空白。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 软件文档对程序理解、入职培训、代码审查和长期维护至关重要，但手动生成文档耗时且易出现不完整或不一致的问题。虽然 LLM 有望通过自动生成自然语言描述来改善这一状况，但其效果高度依赖提示方式，而当前研究对此缺乏系统性理解。

Method: 系统性文献综述方法，对现有研究进行整合、分类和有效性检验。

Result: 识别并分类了多种提示范式（包括少样本提示、思维链推理、检索增强生成和零样本学习），发现现有评估实践差异显著，多数依赖词汇重叠类指标，可能无法捕捉语义质量。

Conclusion: 研究揭示了当前 LLM 提示工程在代码文档任务中的碎片化现状，指出缺乏系统性的比较研究和语义评估标准，并指明了未来研究方向和实践应用路径。

Abstract: Software documentation is essential for program comprehension, developer onboarding, code review, and long-term maintenance. Yet producing quality documentation manually is time-consuming and frequently yields incomplete or inconsistent results. Large language models (LLMs) offer a promising solution by automatically generating natural language descriptions from source code, helping developers understand code more efficiently, facilitating maintenance, and supporting downstream activities such as defect localization and commit message generation. However, the effectiveness of LLMs in documentation tasks critically depends on how they are prompted. Properly structured instructions can substantially improve model performance, making prompt engineering-the design of input prompts to guide model behavior-a foundational technique in LLM-based software engineering. Approaches such as few-shot prompting, chain-of-thought reasoning, retrieval-augmented generation, and zero-shot learning show promise for code summarization, yet current research remains fragmented. There is limited understanding of which prompting strategies work best, for which models, and under what conditions. Moreover, evaluation practices vary widely, with most studies relying on overlap-based metrics that may not capture semantic quality. This systematic literature review consolidates existing evidence, categorizes prompting paradigms, examines their effectiveness, and identifies gaps to guide future research and practical adoption.

</details>


### [82] [Analyzing Chain of Thought (CoT) Approaches in Control Flow Code Deobfuscation Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15390)
*Seyedreza Mohseni,Sarvesh Baskar,Edward Raff,Manas Gaur*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 论文探索将CoT prompting应用于代码去混淆任务，评估了5个SOTA大语言模型，结果表明CoT显著优于简单提示，GPT-5表现最优，能有效辅助代码去混淆并降低逆向工程人工成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 代码去混淆通常需要数天甚至数月的手动工作，需要复杂且昂贵的分析工具，因此需要探索替代方案。

Method: 采用Chain-of-Thought (CoT) prompting方法，引导大语言模型进行显式、逐步的代码分析推理。针对控制流混淆（包括控制流扁平化CFF、模糊谓词及组合混淆）进行评估，使用控制流图结构指标和基于输出相似性的语义指标衡量去混淆效果。

Result: CoT prompting显著提升去混淆质量。GPT-5表现最佳，相比零样本提示，控制流图重建平均提升约16%，语义保持提升约20.5%。

Conclusion: CoT引导的大语言模型可作为代码去混淆的有效辅助工具，提高代码可解释性、控制流图重构的准确性和程序行为的保持度，同时可能降低逆向工程的的人工成本。模型性能不仅取决于混淆级别和混淆器类型，还与原始控制流图的内在复杂度相关。

Abstract: Code deobfuscation is the task of recovering a readable version of a program while preserving its original behavior. In practice, this often requires days or even months of manual work with complex and expensive analysis tools. In this paper, we explore an alternative approach based on Chain-of-Thought (CoT) prompting, where a large language model is guided through explicit, step-by-step reasoning tailored for code analysis. We focus on control flow obfuscation, including Control Flow Flattening (CFF), Opaque Predicates, and their combination, and we measure both structural recovery of the control flow graph and preservation of program semantics. We evaluate five state-of-the-art large language models and show that CoT prompting significantly improves deobfuscation quality compared with simple prompting. We validate our approach on a diverse set of standard C benchmarks and report results using both structural metrics for control flow graphs and semantic metrics based on output similarity. Among the tested models and by applying CoT, GPT5 achieves the strongest overall performance, with an average gain of about 16% in control-flow graph reconstruction and about 20.5% in semantic preservation across our benchmarks compared to zero-shot prompting. Our results also show that model performance depends not only on the obfuscation level and the chosen obfuscator but also on the intrinsic complexity of the original control flow graph. Collectively, these findings suggest that CoT-guided large language models can serve as effective assistants for code deobfuscation, providing improved code explainability, more faithful control flow graph reconstruction, and better preservation of program behavior while potentially reducing the manual effort needed for reverse engineering.

</details>


### [83] [The Semi-Executable Stack: Agentic Software Engineering and the Expanding Scope of SE](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15468)
*Robert Feldt,Per Lenberg,Julian Frattini,Dhasarathy Parthasarathy*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文认为AI对软件工程的真正影响不是使其失去相关性，而是将"工程对象"从确定性执行的可执行代码扩展到依赖人类或概率解释的半可执行制品。为此提出了六环诊断模型"半可执行堆栈"，涵盖可执行工件、指导性工件、编排执行、控制、运营逻辑和社会制度契合度，并开发了"保留vs提纯"启发式方法指导决策。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前围绕AI对软件工程威胁的讨论存在普遍焦虑——从学生到资深从业者都担心专业技能价值会贬值。作者认为这是对技术变革的误读，需要重新理解"被工程化的对象"的本质变化。

Method: 提出了"半可执行堆栈"(Semi-Executable Stack)概念——一个六环诊断参考模型，用于推理软件工程的扩展；该模型涵盖六个层级：可执行工件、指导性工件、编排执行、控制、运营逻辑和社会制度契合度；通过三个实践案例支撑论证，将常见反对意见重新框架为工程目标而非 dismissal 理由。

Result: 扩展了软件工程的关注范围，将半可执行工件（自然语言、工具、工作流、控制机制和组织流程的组合）纳入工程范畴；提供了诊断工具帮助定位问题所在层级及依赖关系；将抽象担忧转化为具体工程目标。

Conclusion: 论文提出"保留与提纯"启发式方法，用于决定哪些遗留软件工程流程、控制和协调机制应保留、简化或重新设计；该模型还提供了六环诊断参考框架，帮助定位贡献、瓶颈或组织转型所在的位置及其依赖的相邻层级。

Abstract: AI-based systems, currently driven largely by LLMs and tool-using agentic harnesses, are increasingly discussed as a possible threat to software engineering. Foundation models get stronger, agents can plan and act across multiple steps, and tasks such as scaffolding, routine test generation, straightforward bug fixing, and small integration work look more exposed than they did only a few years ago. The result is visible unease not only among students and junior developers, but also among experienced practitioners who worry that hard-won expertise may lose value. This paper argues for a different reading. The important shift is not that software engineering loses relevance. It is that the thing being engineered expands beyond executable code to semi-executable artifacts; combinations of natural language, tools, workflows, control mechanisms, and organizational routines whose enactment depends on human or probabilistic interpretation rather than deterministic execution. The Semi-Executable Stack is introduced as a six-ring diagnostic reference model for reasoning about that expansion, spanning executable artifacts, instructional artifacts, orchestrated execution, controls, operating logic, and societal and institutional fit. The model helps locate where a contribution, bottleneck, or organizational transition primarily sits, and which adjacent rings it depends on. The paper develops the argument through three worked cases, reframes familiar objections as engineering targets rather than reasons to dismiss the transition, and closes with a preserve-versus-purify heuristic for deciding which legacy software engineering processes, controls, and coordination routines should be kept and which should be simplified or redesigned. This paper is a conceptual keynote companion: diagnostic and agenda-setting rather than empirical.

</details>


### [84] [QMutBench: A Dataset of Quantum Circuit Mutants](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15870)
*Eñaut Mendiluze Usandizaga,Thomas Laurent,Paolo Arcaini,Shaukat Ali*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: QMutBench是一个量子软件变异体基准数据集，包含超过700,000个量子电路变异体，通过在线接口提供灵活的筛选功能，帮助开发者和测试人员评估测试用例质量、比较不同测试技术，并促进新的变异引导量子软件测试技术的研发。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子软件测试领域缺乏包含故障的基准数据集，导致无法有效评估测试用例检测故障的能力。

Method: QMutBench通过生成超过700,000个量子电路变异体来构建数据集，提供在线接口供用户根据原始量子电路、期望存活率、变异类型（如故障量子门类型）等选择标准获取所需的变异体基准。

Result: QMutBench数据集包含超过700,000个代表不同故障的量子电路变异体，支持多种选择标准，提供可访问的在线接口。

Conclusion: QMutBench为量子软件开发者和测试人员提供了一个可访问的在线基准数据集，可用于评估测试用例质量或比较不同测试技术，同时支持开发新的变异引导量子软件测试技术。

Abstract: Quantum software testing has attracted interest in recent years, prompting the development of various techniques to automate the testing of quantum software. These techniques generate test cases that must be assessed for their effectiveness in detecting faults. Such an assessment requires benchmarks of faulty programs. However, there is a lack of benchmarks containing faults. In this data showcase, we propose QMutBench, a dataset that contains over 700,000 quantum circuit mutants representing different faults. The dataset is accessible via an online interface with selection criteria, such as the original quantum circuit(s) from which mutants are generated, the desired survival rate of the selected mutants, and other mutation characteristics (e.g., the type of faulty quantum gate). QMutBench provides quantum software developers and testers with an accessible online dataset to obtain benchmarks of mutants necessary to assess either the quality of the test cases generated by their testing technique or to compare different testing techniques. It also enables the development of new mutation-guided quantum software testing techniques.

</details>


### [85] [LLM4C2Rust: Large Language Models for Automated Memory-Safe Code Transpilation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15485)
*Sarah Bedell,Nazanin Siavash,Armin Moin*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 提出一种基于检索增强生成(RAG)框架的C/C++到Rust自动转译方法，通过结合LLM与SLM处理代码块，并利用Rust文档和编译器错误参考进行上下文引导，旨在提升转译代码的内存安全性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统C/C++代码存在内存安全隐患，手动转译为Rust既耗时又易出错。基于规则的自动转译方法缺乏灵活性，而大语言模型(LLM)技术为解决此问题提供了新途径。

Method: 提出RAG辅助框架，将LLM与小型语言模型(SLM)集成，采用分段策略处理C/C++代码块，并通过Rust文档和编译器错误参考进行上下文检索引导。使用GPT-4o、GPT-4-Turbo和o3-Mini三个OpenAI模型进行实验验证。

Result: RAG增强管道在C到Rust代码转译中总体上提升了代码正确性和安全性。多个Coreutils程序实现了原始指针解引用(RPD)和不安全类型转换(UTC)的完全消除。

Conclusion: 基于LLM的转译方法在自动化软件现代化和内存安全代码生成方面具有显著潜力。

Abstract: Memory safety has long been a critical challenge in software engineering, particularly for legacy systems written in memory-unsafe languages such as C and C++. Rust, one of the youngest modern programming languages, offers built-in memory-safety guarantees that make it a strong candidate for secure systems development. Consequently, transpiling C/C++ code into memory-safe Rust code has become a growing area of research. However, manual transpilation is often time-consuming and error-prone. Additionally, rule-based automated approaches are not as flexible or cost-effective as methods enabled by state-of-the-art AI models, techniques, and methods, such as those that deploy Large Language Models (LLMs), for example, Generative Pretrained Transformers (GPT). In this paper, we propose a Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG)-assisted framework that integrates an LLM with a Small Language Model (SLM) to perform C/C++-to-Rust transpilation with a focus on enhancing memory safety. The framework deploys a segmentation strategy that processes C/C++ code in balanced blocks, guiding the LLM with retrieved context from Rust documentation and compiler error references. Our experiments using three OpenAI models (GPT-4o, GPT-4-Turbo, and o3-Mini) demonstrate that the RAG-enhanced pipeline generally improves both code correctness and security for C-to-Rust code transpilation. Several Coreutils programs achieve complete elimination of Raw Pointer Dereferences (RPDs) and Unsafe Type Casts (UTCs) in the final Rust output, indicating the potential of LLM-based transpilation for advancing automated software modernization and repair, as well as memory-safe code generation.

</details>


### [86] [Empirical Investigation of Quantum Computing Toolchains and Algorithms : Mining Stack Overflow Repository](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15512)
*Maryam Tavassoli Sabzevari,Arif Ali Khan*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本研究通过分析1,404条Stack Overflow帖子研究量子计算领域的开发者讨论，采用主题建模和定量分析识别主要讨论话题、流行工具和算法，并评估问题难度。研究发现7个主要主题，混合量子-经典计算和量子电路实现最为流行，Qiskit和Q#主导讨论，Grover和Shor算法最常被引用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子计算正从研究阶段向实际工业应用过渡，亟需了解开发者如何 engagement with 量子技术，现有研究对此领域开发者实际讨论和问题的实证分析不足。

Method: 分析1,404条Stack Overflow上与量子计算相关的帖子，采用主题建模（topic modeling）和定量分析方法，识别主要讨论话题、使用的工具和编程语言、量子算法；使用两个指标评估问题难度：无 accepted answer 的问题比例和获得 accepted answer 的中位时间。

Result: 发现7个主要讨论主题；混合量子-经典计算和量子电路实现最为流行；Qiskit和Q#是主导工具；Grover和Shor算法最常被引用；不同主题、工具和算法的问题难度和社区参与度存在显著差异，表明各领域成熟度和社区支持水平不同。

Conclusion: 研究为量子软件工程领域的研究者、工具开发者和教育者提供了可操作的洞察，有助于改进量子工具的可用性、文档和学习资源；数据集已在Zenodo公开以支持透明性和可复现性。

Abstract: Quantum computing (QC) is increasingly transitioning toward practical and industrial adoption, highlighting the need to understand how developers engage with quantum technologies. In this study, we analyze 1,404 Stack Overflow posts related to quantum computing topics, including quantum programming, tools, and algorithms, to investigate real-world developer discussions. Using topic modeling and quantitative analysis, we identify the main discussion topics, their popularity, and the tools, programming languages, and quantum algorithms referenced by practitioners. We further assess the difficulty of developer questions using two metrics: (i) the percentage of questions without accepted answers and (ii) the median time required to receive an accepted answer. Our findings reveal seven main topics, with hybrid quantum--classical computing and quantum circuit implementation emerging as the most prevalent. We observe that Qiskit and Q-sharp dominate developer discussions, while Grover's and Shor's algorithms are the most frequently referenced. Moreover, our analysis highlights differences in engagement and difficulty across topics, tools, and algorithms, indicating varying levels of maturity and community support. These findings provide actionable insights for researchers, tool developers, and educators, supporting improvements in usability, documentation, and learning resources in quantum software engineering.
