To support transparency and reproducibility, the open-source dataset used in this study is publicly available at Zenodo.

</details>


### [87] [Symbolic Guardrails for Domain-Specific Agents: Stronger Safety and Security Guarantees Without Sacrificing Utility](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15579)
*Yining Hong,Yining She,Eunsuk Kang,Christopher S. Timperley,Christian Kästner*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文研究符号防护栏作为为AI智能体提供强安全保证的实用路径，通过系统综述80个SOTA基准测试、分析符号防护栏的保证能力边界、以及多基准实验评估，证明符号防护栏能有效提升AI智能体的安全与安全性，且不损失实用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在AI智能体与环境的交互应用中，高风险商业场景下的意外操作可能导致隐私泄露和财务损失等不可接受的危害。现有缓解方法（如训练方法和神经防护栏）无法提供保证，亟需一种能够提供强安全保证的实用方法。

Method: 三部分研究：(1) 系统综述80个SOTA智能体安全与安全基准测试；(2) 分析哪些策略需求可由符号防护栏保证；(3) 在τ²-Bench、CAR-bench和MedAgentBench上评估符号防护栏对安全、安全性和智能体成功率的影响。

Result: 85%的基准测试缺乏具体策略；74%的指定策略需求可由符号防护栏执行；符号防护栏在提升安全性的同时不牺牲智能体效用。

Conclusion: 符号防护栏是一种实用且有效的方法来保证AI智能体的安全和安全要求，尤其适用于特定领域的AI智能体。

Abstract: AI agents that interact with their environments through tools enable powerful applications, but in high-stakes business settings, unintended actions can cause unacceptable harm, such as privacy breaches and financial loss. Existing mitigations, such as training-based methods and neural guardrails, improve agent reliability but cannot provide guarantees. We study symbolic guardrails as a practical path toward strong safety and security guarantees for AI agents. Our three-part study includes a systematic review of 80 state-of-the-art agent safety and security benchmarks to identify the policies they evaluate, an analysis of which policy requirements can be guaranteed by symbolic guardrails, and an evaluation of how symbolic guardrails affect safety, security, and agent success on $τ^2$-Bench, CAR-bench, and MedAgentBench. We find that 85\% of benchmarks lack concrete policies, relying instead on underspecified high-level goals or common sense. Among the specified policies, 74\% of policy requirements can be enforced by symbolic guardrails, often using simple, low-cost mechanisms. These guardrails improve safety and security without sacrificing agent utility. Overall, our results suggest that symbolic guardrails are a practical and effective way to guarantee some safety and security requirements, especially for domain-specific AI agents. We release all codes and artifacts at https://github.com/hyn0027/agent-symbolic-guardrails.

</details>


### [88] [CodeMMR: Bridging Natural Language, Code, and Image for Unified Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15663)
*Jiahui Geng,Qing Li,Fengyu Cai,Fakhri Karray*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: MMCoIR是首个评估多模态代码检索的全面基准，涵盖5个视觉领域、8种编程语言、11个代码库。在此基础上提出的CodeMMR模型通过指令驱动的多模态对齐联合嵌入文本、代码和图像，在检索任务上显著优于现有基线，并将多模态检索能力成功集成到RAG系统中提升代码生成质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有代码IR模型大多以文本为中心，忽视了编程工件中固有的视觉和结构特征（如Web界面、数据可视化、SVG、示意图、UML等），限制了代码发现、重用和LLM编码辅助的效果。

Method: 通过基于指令的多模态对齐，将自然语言、代码和图像联合嵌入到共享语义空间中，实现跨模态和跨语言的统一检索能力。

Result: CodeMMR在nDCG@10指标上平均超越基线模型（如UniIR、GME、VLM2Vec）约10个百分点；在RAG场景中提升了代码生成保真度和视觉基础能力。

Conclusion: CodeMMR在RAG中展示了多模态检索的潜力，可增强代码生成保真度和视觉基础能力，指向下一代智能编程系统的发展方向。

Abstract: Code search, framed as information retrieval (IR), underpins modern software engineering and increasingly powers retrieval-augmented generation (RAG), improving code discovery, reuse, and the reliability of LLM-based coding. Yet existing code IR models remain largely text-centric and often overlook the visual and structural aspects inherent in programming artifacts such as web interfaces, data visualizations, SVGs, schematic diagrams, and UML. To bridge this gap, we introduce MMCoIR, the first comprehensive benchmark for evaluating multimodal code IR across five visual domains, eight programming languages, eleven libraries, and show the challenge of the task through extensive evaluation. Therefore, we then propose CodeMMR, a unified retrieval model that jointly embeds natural language, code, and images into a shared semantic space through instruction-based multimodal alignment. CodeMMR achieves strong generalization across modalities and languages, outperforming competitive baselines (e.g., UniIR, GME, VLM2Vec) by an average of 10 points on nDCG@10. Moreover, integrating CodeMMR into RAG enhances code generation fidelity and visual grounding on unseen code generation tasks, underscoring the potential of multimodal retrieval as a core enabler for next-generation intelligent programming systems. Datasets are available at HuggingFace.

</details>


### [89] [Feature Toggle Dynamics in Large-Scale Systems: Prevalence, Growth, Lifespan, and Benchmarking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15872)
*Xhevahire Tërnava*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文对Kubernetes（10 MLoC，8.5年）和GitLab（5 MLoC，5年）两个大型开源项目中的4000余条功能开关事件进行了纵向研究，发现功能开关的移除滞后于添加（分别约35%和13%），导致开关库存持续增长。Kubernetes开关中位生命周期为734天，GitLab为185天。部分开关（分别为1.33%和0.73%）成为事实上的永久开关。基于这些发现，文章提出了包含五个关键指标及其经验阈值的基准测试框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 功能开关虽能实现渐进式发布和实验，但常超出预期生命周期而累积为技术债务。现有研究已探讨了功能开关的交互和复杂性，但缺乏跨不同组织情境量化开关演变过程的纵向研究。

Method: 对两个大型开源项目（Kubernetes和GitLab）中的超过4000条功能开关事件进行纵向分析，通过代码仓库历史数据追踪开关的添加、移除及存续时间等生命周期特征。

Result: 发现功能开关的移除滞后于添加速度（Kubernetes约35%，GitLab约13%），导致开关库存不断增长；两个系统的开关生命周期差异显著（Kubernetes中位数734天 vs GitLab 185天）；部分开关（1.33%和0.73%）超过了所有先前观察到的移除时长，成为事实上的永久开关。

Conclusion: 研究提出了一个包含五个关键指标及经验阈值的功能开关管理基准测试框架，使实践者能够评估和比较项目间的开关管理实践。所有脚本和数据已公开。

Abstract: Feature toggles enable gradual rollouts and experimentation in software systems, yet often persist beyond their intended lifecycle, accumulating as technical debt. Prior research has examined feature toggle interactions and complexity, but no longitudinal study has quantified how toggles evolve over time across different organizational contexts. We analyse over 4,000 toggle events in Kubernetes (10 MLoC, 8.5 years) and GitLab (5 MLoC, 5 years). We find that feature toggle removals lags behind additions in both systems (by roughly 35% and 13%, respectively), leading to growing toggle inventories. Their lifespan patterns also differ notably, with Kubernetes toggles lasting a median of 734 days versus 185 in GitLab. Then, some feature toggles (1.33% and 0.73%, respectively) exceed all previously observed removal durations, becoming de facto permanent. Building on these findings, we propose a benchmarking framework with five key metrics and their empirically derived threshold zones, enabling practitioners to assess and compare toggle management practices across projects. All scripts and data are publicly available.

</details>


### [90] [Small Yet Configurable: Unveiling Null Variability in Software](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15957)
*Xhevahire Tërnava,Georges Aaron Randrianaina,Luc Lesoil,Mathieu Acher*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 首次实证研究small-scale软件系统的可配置性，分析108个GNU coreutils程序，发现小型程序也具有高达76个选项的高可变性，且运行时可变性与代码库规模高度相关（0.78），随时间推移可变性主要因编译时可变性而增加。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 小型软件系统在日常任务中广泛使用，但其可配置性尚未得到充分探索。同时，现代软件系统呈现出可配置性不断增强的趋势，学界也对构建不可变、专业化和可复现软件越来越感兴趣。

Method: 实证研究方法，分析了来自GNU coreutils的108个程序，并对20个最小的程序进行了跨85个版本的纵向分析。

Result: 发现小型程序也能展现出显著的编译时和运行时可变性，每个程序最多可达76个选项。运行时可变性与代码库规模之间存在高度相关性（0.78）。对20个最小程序的85个版本分析显示，可变性随时间推移呈增长趋势，主要由编译时可变性增加所致。

Conclusion: 高可配置性并非大型系统独有特征。减少不必要的可变性可导致更轻量、更小、更易维护的软件。提出null-variable软件系统概念（无强制功能外的可配置性）。建议通过在编译时和运行时之间转换选项、移除不必要的运行时可变性或提前解决编译时可变性来减少代码库复杂性和规模。

Abstract: Many small-scale software systems, that is, with limited codebase or binary size, are widely used in everyday tasks, yet their configurability remains largely unexplored. At the same time, studies on modern software systems show a trend toward increasing configurability, alongside growing interest in building immutable, specialized, and reproducible software. In this paper, we present the first empirical study on the extent of configurability in small-scale software systems. By analyzing 108 programs from GNU coreutils, we show that even small programs can exhibit significant compile-time and run-time variability, with up to 76 options per program. Then, there is a high correlation (0.78) between run-time variability and codebase size. Furthermore, an analysis of the 20 smallest programs across 85 releases reveals that variability tends to increase over time, primarily due to the added compile-time variability. This suggests that shifting options between run-time and compile-time, removing unnecessary run-time variability, or resolving compile-time variability early, can help reduce codebase complexity and size. We also introduce, for the first time, the concept of null-variable software system, one with no configurability beyond mandatory features. Our findings show that high configurability is not exclusive to large-scale systems and that reducing unnecessary variability can lead to lightweight, smaller, and more maintainable software. We hope this effort contributes to designing new software by understanding how to balance its configurability with codebase size.

</details>


### [91] [Supporting the Comprehension of Data Analysis Scripts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15963)
*Florian Sihler,Oliver Gerstl,Lars Pfrenger,Julian Schubert,Matthias Tichy*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: flowR是一个面向R语言数据分析师的IDE扩展（支持Positron和VS Code），通过构建数据流图、提供交互式可视化和linting等功能，改善数据清洗和可视化脚本的可理解性和可维护性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数据清洗和可视化脚本难以理解和维护，导致可重复性和可复用性受阻；同时缺乏针对此类脚本的工具支持。

Method: flowR通过将过程间数据流和控制流分析与增量式分析相结合，为R项目构建全面的数据流图（Dataflow Graph），并集成了静态向后程序切片、交互式图形可视化、linting和内联值注解等功能。插件系统允许集成进一步的静态分析功能。

Result: flowR能够增量分析R项目，充分利用R的动态特性和探索式编程范式，为数据分析人员提供项目概览、交互式图形可视化、linting检查和内联值注解等功能支持。

Conclusion: flowR实现了近乎实时的反馈，平均576ms即可计算真实世界项目的完整数据流图。提供了插件系统和接口，支持集成额外的分析功能如新的linting规则或自定义可视化。

Abstract: A lot of research relies on data analysis scripts to process, clean, and visualize data. However, recent studies show that these scripts are often hard to comprehend and maintain, hindering reproducibility and reuse, accompanied by a lack of tool support for handling such scripts. In this work, we focus on the R programming language, addressing this problem by presenting flowR as an extension for the common data analysis IDEs Positron and VS Code. Alongside a previously presented static backward program slicer, flowR provides an overview of data analysis scripts, interactive graph visualizations, linting, and inline value annotations to support data analysts. FlowR incrementally analyzes R projects by intertwining interprocedural data- and control-flow analyses to build a comprehensive dataflow graph, incorporating R's dynamic and explorative features. Additionally, flowR offers a plugin system and interfaces, allowing the integration of further analyses, such as new linting rules or custom visualizations. Requiring an average of 576ms to calculate the full dataflow graph of real-world projects, this enables near real-time feedback. The demonstration video is available at  https://youtu.be/hJzr-r-NmMg . For the full source code and extensive documentation, refer to https://github.com/flowr-analysis/flowr . To try the docker image, use `docker run --rm -it eagleoutice/flowr`.

</details>


### [92] [Neurosymbolic Repo-level Code Localization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16021)
*Xiufeng Xu,Xiufeng Wu,Zejun Zhang,Yi Li*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: Summary generation failed


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: Code localization is a cornerstone of autonomous software engineering. Recent advancements have achieved impressive performance on real-world issue benchmarks. However, we identify a critical yet overlooked bias: these benchmarks are saturated with keyword references (e.g. file paths, function names), encouraging models to rely on superficial lexical matching rather than genuine structural reasoning. We term this phenomenon the Keyword Shortcut. To address this, we formalize the challenge of Keyword-Agnostic Logical Code Localization (KA-LCL) and introduce KA-LogicQuery, a diagnostic benchmark requiring structural reasoning without any naming hints. Our evaluation reveals a catastrophic performance drop of state-of-the-art approaches on KA-LogicQuery, exposing their lack of deterministic reasoning capabilities. We propose LogicLoc, a novel agentic framework that combines large language models with the rigorous logical reasoning of Datalog for precise localization. LogicLoc extracts program facts from the codebase and leverages an LLM to synthesize Datalog programs, with parser-gated validation and mutation-based intermediate-rule diagnostic feedback to ensure correctness and efficiency. The validated programs are executed by a high-performance inference engine, enabling accurate and verifiable localization in a fully automated, closed-loop workflow. Experimental results demonstrate that LogicLoc significantly outperforms SOTA methods on KA-LogicQuery while maintaining competitive performance on popular issue-driven benchmarks. Notably, LogicLoc attains superior performance with significantly lower token consumption and faster execution by offloading structural traversal to a deterministic engine, reducing the overhead of iterative LLM inference.

</details>


### [93] [LLMSniffer: Detecting LLM-Generated Code via GraphCodeBERT and Supervised Contrastive Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16058)
*Mahir Labib Dihan,Abir Muhtasim*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: LLMSniffer是一个基于GraphCodeBERT的AI生成代码检测框架，通过两阶段监督对比学习和注释去除预处理，在两个基准数据集上显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型(LLM)在软件开发中的快速普及，使得区分AI生成代码与人类编写代码成为关键挑战，涉及学术诚信、代码质量保证和软件安全等方面。

Method: 基于GraphCodeBERT微调，采用两阶段监督对比学习pipeline、注释去除预处理和MLP分类器。

Result: 在GPTSniffer上准确率从70%提升至78%，F1从68%升至78%；在Whodunit上准确率从91%提升至94.65%，F1从91%升至94.64%。t-SNE可视化验证了嵌入质量。

Conclusion: 提出的对比学习微调方法显著优于基线方法，并开源了模型、数据集和代码以促进后续研究。

Abstract: The rapid proliferation of Large Language Models (LLMs) in software development has made distinguishing AI-generated code from human-written code a critical challenge with implications for academic integrity, code quality assurance, and software security. We present LLMSniffer, a detection framework that fine-tunes GraphCodeBERT using a two-stage supervised contrastive learning pipeline augmented with comment removal preprocessing and an MLP classifier. Evaluated on two benchmark datasets - GPTSniffer and Whodunit - LLMSniffer achieves substantial improvements over prior baselines: accuracy increases from 70% to 78% on GPTSniffer (F1: 68% to 78%) and from 91% to 94.65% on Whodunit (F1: 91% to 94.64%). t-SNE visualizations confirm that contrastive fine-tuning yields well-separated, compact embeddings. We release our model checkpoints, datasets, codes and a live interactive demo to facilitate further research.

</details>


### [94] [Bridging the Gap between User Intent and LLM: A Requirement Alignment Approach for Code Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16198)
*Jia Li,Ruiqi Bai,Yangkang Luo,Yiran Zhang,Wentao Yang,Zeyu Sun,Tiankuo Zhao,Dongming Jin,Lei Li,Zhi Jin*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 论文提出了REA-Coder，一个通过需求对齐来增强LLM代码生成能力的方法。该方法通过识别和对齐LLM难以理解的需求内容，并迭代执行需求对齐与代码生成过程来解决传统方法忽视的"需求理解"问题。实验表明该方法在多个LLM和数据集上均取得了显著性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有代码生成方法主要关注设计推理策略或后修正方法来提升代码生成性能，但这些方法都基于一个共同假设：LLM能正确理解给定的需求。然而，这个假设并不总是成立。

Method: REA-Coder首先识别LLM不理解的需求内容并进行对齐，然后基于对齐后的需求生成代码，并验证生成的代码是否与需求一致，迭代执行需求对齐和代码生成过程直到生成正确代码或达到最大迭代次数。在4个LLM和5个编程基准数据集上验证了该方法的有效性。

Result: REA-Coder在4个LLM和5个编程基准数据集上优于所有先进基线方法，具体在各数据集上分别实现了7.93%、30.25%、26.75%、8.59%和8.64%的平均提升。

Conclusion: REA-Coder通过需求对齐机制有效提升LLM代码生成性能，在5个编程基准数据集上平均提升7.93%~30.25%，证明需求对齐是提升代码生成能力的关键途径。

Abstract: Code generation refers to automatically producing executable programs from user requirements. Recently, researchers have explored approaches to enhance the correctness of generated code with advanced large language models. Although achieving improvements, existing approaches focus on designing reasoning strategies or post-refinement methods to enhance code generation performance. Despite their differences, all these methods share a common assumption: the LLM can correctly understand the given requirement. However, this assumption does not always hold. To fill this gap, we propose REA-Coder, a requirement alignment approach to enhance the code generation performance of LLMs. REA-Coder involves first identifying the requirement content that does not align with LLMs and aligning the requirements. Then, based on the aligned requirements, LLMs generate code and further verify whether the generated code aligns with the requirements, iterating this process of requirement alignment and code generation until generating correct code or achieving the maximum number of iterations. Experimental results show that REA-Coder outperforms all advanced baselines on four LLMs across five programming benchmarks. Concretely, REA-Coder achieves average improvements of 7.93%, 30.25%, 26.75%, 8.59%, and 8.64% on the five benchmark datasets, demonstrating the effectiveness of requirement alignment for improving the code generation performance of LLMs.

</details>


### [95] [From Papers to Progress: Rethinking Knowledge Accumulation in Software Engineering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16208)
*Jason Cusati,Chris Brown*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文通过调查280名资深研究人员，分析软件工程领域的知识积累问题。识别出四个结构性障碍：论文孤立性、出版流程中上下文丢失、Claims 演化无追踪、激励偏向 novelty 而非整合。提出四个技术无关的研究 artifact 设计原则，为 FOSE 作为新型 artifact 实验平台铺路。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 软件工程领域虽研究产出增长迅速，但知识积累、整合和复用的能力不足，难以支持长期科学进步

Method: 通过对280名全球分布的高经验研究人员的 ICSE 2026 FOSE 预调查问卷分析

Result: 诊断出四个相互关联的结构性问题：论文作为孤立知识单元、语境与溯源丢失、Claims 演化缺乏追踪、激励结构偏向 novelty

Conclusion: 论文认为解决知识积累障碍需要重新思考研究 artifact 的基本属性，并提出 ICSE/FOSE 可作为实验新型 artifact 设计的平台

Abstract: Software engineering research has experienced rapid growth in both output and participation over the past decades. Yet concerns persist about the field's ability to accumulate, integrate, and reuse knowledge in ways that support long-term progress. To better understand how the community itself perceives these challenges, we analyze responses from the ICSE 2026 Future of Software Engineering pre-survey, which captures perspectives from 280 globally distributed and highly experienced researchers. Our analysis reveals a tension between increasing research productivity and the limited mechanisms available for synthesizing results, tracking evolving claims, and supporting cumulative understanding over time.
  Building on these observations, we diagnose four interrelated structural breakdowns: papers function as isolated knowledge units with claims embedded in prose; context and provenance are lost as knowledge moves through the publication pipeline; claims evolve without systematic tracking; and incentive structures favor novelty over consolidation. We argue that addressing these barriers requires rethinking the fundamental properties of research artifacts.
  We articulate four technology-agnostic principles for future research artifacts: structured and interpretable representations of claims and evidence; inspectable and provenance-aware documentation of methodological decisions; long-lived and reusable substrates that evolve beyond publication; and governance mechanisms that align individual incentives with collective knowledge-building goals. We discuss implications for research practice, publication norms, and community infrastructure, positioning FOSE as a venue for experimenting with alternative artifact designs that support cumulative scientific progress.

</details>


<div id='cs.DS'></div>

# cs.DS [[Back]](#toc)

### [96] [The Communication Complexity of Pattern Matching with Edits Revisited](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15601)
*Tomasz Kociumaka,Jakob Nogler,Philip Wellnitz*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 研究Pattern Matching with Edits问题的通信复杂度，改进编码大小上界至O(n/m · k log(m|Σ|/k)) bits，实现与下界的匹配，并建立了编辑序列报告变体的新紧下界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 改进STOC'24论文中的编码上界，解决上界与下界之间存在对数因子的差距问题。

Method: 重新审视原始证明，优化编码构造。

Result: 编码大小从O(n/m · k log² m)改进到O(n/m · k log(m|Σ|/k))，在常数字母表情况下与下界匹配。新建立Ω(n/m · k log(m|Σ|/k))的紧下界。

Conclusion: 完美解决了编码上界问题，建立了编辑序列报告变体的紧界，并揭示了与mismatches问题streaming复杂度的联系。

Abstract: In the decades-old Pattern Matching with Edits problem, given a length-$n$ string $T$ (the text), a length-$m$ string $P$ (the pattern), and a positive integer $k$ (the threshold), the task is to list the $k$-error occurrences of $P$ in $T$, that is, all fragments of $T$ whose edit distance to $P$ is at most $k$. The one-way communication complexity of Pattern Matching with Edits is the minimum number of bits that Alice, given an instance $(P, T, k)$ of the problem, must send to Bob so that Bob can reconstruct the answer solely from that message.
  For the natural parameter regime of $0 < k < m < n/2$, our recent work [STOC'24] yields that $Ω(n/m \cdot k \log(m/k))$ bits are necessary and $O(n/m \cdot k \log^2 m)$ bits are sufficient for Pattern Matching with Edits. More generally, for strings over an alphabet $Σ$, our recent work [STOC'24] gives an $O(n/m \cdot k \log m \log(m|Σ|))$-bit encoding that allows one to recover a shortest sequence of edits for every $k$-error occurrence of $P$ in $T$.
  In this work, we revisit the original proof and improve the encoding size to $O(n/m \cdot k \log(m|Σ|/k))$, which matches the lower bound for constant-sized alphabets. We further establish a new tight lower bound of $Ω(n/m \cdot k \log(m|Σ|/k))$ for the edit sequence reporting variant that we solve. Our encoding size also matches the communication complexity established for the simpler Pattern Matching with Mismatches problem in the context of streaming algorithms [Clifford, Kociumaka, Porat; SODA'19].

</details>


### [97] [Backdoors for Quantified Boolean Formulas](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15927)
*Leif Eriksson,Victor Lagerkvist,Sebastian Ordyniak,George Osipov,Fahad Panolan,Mateusz Rychlicki*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了QBF相对于最大可追踪句法类（HORN、2-SAT、AFFINE）的后门参数化复杂度，证明仅用常数规模后门时QBF仍保持PSpace难，但通过额外参数化量词深度可为2-SAT和AFFINE获得FPT算法，并引入增强型后门框架以覆盖结构化限制如入射树宽度，开发了相关检测和求解的FPT算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SAT问题通过利用隐藏的结构性质（如朝向可追踪性的后门）取得了实际成功，但QBF的后门分析相对有限。由于QBF是PSpace完全问题，自然要探索PSpace中的问题是否也能像SAT那样受益于后门驱动的求解方法，因此需要系统研究QBF的后门参数化复杂度。

Method: 采用参数化复杂度理论方法，对QBF进行系统性研究。具体包括：（1）定义增强型后门——在原始图中至多k个顶点的分离集，使得该分离集与所有纯粹全称分量中包含的变量共同构成后门；（2）设计FPT算法处理增强型后门下的求解和检测问题；（3）通过归约和复杂性下界证明完成分类。

Result: 主要结果包括：（1）证明了即使对于常数规模的HORN、2-SAT和AFFINE后门，QBF仍保持PSpace难性；（2）当同时参数化量词深度时，2-SAT和AFFINE后门类别的QBF可在FPT时间内求解；（3）3-HORN后门被证明是W[1]难度的；（4）对于所有列出的QBF可追踪类别，增强型后门的检测和求解均可设计出关于参数k的FPT算法。

Conclusion: 通过引入增强型后门框架，将参数化复杂度分析扩展到结构化限制（如有界入射树宽度和量词深度），并为所有上述可追踪类别的增强型后门检测和求解设计了FPT算法，显著扩展了QBF后门的适用性。

Abstract: The quantified Boolean formula problem (QBF) is a well-known PSpace-complete problem with rich expressive power, and is generally viewed as the SAT analogue for PSpace. Given that many problems today are solved in practice by reducing to SAT, and then using highly optimized SAT solvers, it is natural to ask whether problems in PSpace are amenable to this approach. While SAT solvers exploit hidden structural properties, such as backdoors to tractability, backdoor analysis for QBF is comparatively very limited.
  We present a comprehensive study of the (parameterized) complexity of QBF parameterized by backdoor size to the largest tractable syntactic classes: HORN, 2-SAT, and AFFINE. While SAT is in FPT under this parameterization, we prove that QBF remains PSpace-hard even on formulas with backdoors of constant size. Parameterizing additionally by the quantifier depth, we design FPT-algorithms for the classes 2-SAT and AFFINE, and show that 3-HORN is W[1]-hard.
  As our next contribution, we vastly extend the applicability of QBF backdoors not only for the syntactic classes defined above but also for tractable classes defined via structural restrictions, such as formulas with bounded incidence treewidth and quantifier depth. To this end, we introduce enhanced backdoors: these are separators S of size at most k in the primal graph such that S together with all variables contained in any purely universal component of the primal graph minus S is a backdoor. We design FPT-algorithms with respect to k for both evaluation and detection of enhanced backdoors to all tractable classes of QBF listed above and more.

</details>


### [98] [Hardness, Tractability and Density Thresholds of finite Pinwheel Scheduling Variants](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16030)
*Sotiris Kanellopoulos,Giorgos Mitropoulos,Christos Pergaminelis,Thanos Tolias*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 本文研究k-Visits问题（Pinwheel调度的有限版本），证明了2-Visits在最大多重度为2时是强NP完全问题，并给出在常数个不同截止日期时属于RP的算法。同时建立了2-Visits的密度阈值下界√2-1/2≈0.9142，并证明当k→∞时阈值趋近于5/6≈0.8333。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: k-Visits是Kanellopoulos等人在SODA 2026提出的Pinwheel调度有限版本问题。存在两个开放问题：（1）2-Visits在何种条件下是难解的；（2）以不同截止日期数量为参数的参数化复杂性。

Method: （1）通过从3-Partition的归约证明强NP完全性；（2）使用随机化算法证明在常数个不同截止日期时属于RP；（3）通过混合版本（部分任务执行1次，部分执行2次）推广现有正面结果；（4）通过密度阈值分析建立√2-1/2的下界，并证明渐近上界趋近于5/6。

Result: （1）2-Visits在最大多重度=2时强NP完全；（2）常数个不同截止日期时2-Visits∈RP；（3）正面结果推广至混合版本；（4）2-Visits密度阈值≥√2-1/2≈0.9142；k→∞时阈值→5/6≈0.8333。

Conclusion: 解决了SODA 2026的开放问题，确认2-Visits即使受限也是难的；同时为常数截止日期情形提供了有效算法；密度阈值研究补充了Pinwheel调度中5/6阈值的对应结果；并为3-Visits的复杂性提供负面证据。

Abstract: The k-Visits problem is a recently introduced finite version of Pinwheel Scheduling [Kanellopoulos et al., SODA 2026]. Given the deadlines of n tasks, the problem asks whether there exists a schedule of length kn executing each task exactly k times, with no deadline expiring between consecutive visits (executions) of each task.
  In this work we prove that 2-Visits is strongly NP-complete even when the maximum multiplicity of the input is equal to 2, settling an open question from [Kanellopoulos et al., SODA 2026] and contrasting the tractability of 2-Visits for simple sets. On the other hand, we prove that 2-Visits is in RP when the number of distinct deadlines is constant, thus making progress on another open question regarding the parameterization of 2-Visits by the number of numbers. We then generalize all existing positive results for 2-Visits to a version of the problem where some tasks must be visited once and some other tasks twice, while providing evidence that some of these results are unlikely to transfer to 3-Visits.
  Lastly, we establish bounds for the density thresholds of k-Visits, analogous to the $(5/6)$-threshold of Pinwheel Scheduling [Kawamura, STOC 2024]; in particular, we show a $\sqrt{2}-1/2\approx 0.9142$ lower bound for the density threshold of 2-Visits and prove that the density threshold of k-Visits approaches $5/6\approx 0.8333$ for $k \to \infty$.

</details>


### [99] [Constant-Factor Approximations for Doubly Constrained Fair k-Center, k-Median and k-Means](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16061)
*Nicole Funk,Annika Hennes,Johanna Hillebrand,Sarah Sturm*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 本文研究离散k-聚类问题在两种公平约束下的近似算法。对于k-center问题，将近似因子从8改进到4；对于k-median和k-means，提出了首个常数因子近似算法。采用LP方法将满足中心多样性约束的解转换为双重约束公平聚类，并可推广到拟阵和背包约束。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有双重约束公平聚类算法的近似保证较差（8-近似），且缺乏k-median和k-means的有效近似算法。需要改进近似因子并为更多目标函数提供理论保证。

Method: 基于LP的转换方法：将满足中心多样性约束的解通过LP技术转换为满足双重约束的公平聚类解；结合Jones等人(2020)的DS-fair k-center算法。

Result: k-center达到4-近似（带小量加性违反）；k-median和k-means获得首个常数因子近似算法；结果可推广到拟阵k-聚类和背包约束。

Conclusion: 通过LP方法成功改进了双重约束公平聚类的近似比，为多种中心选择约束提供了统一的算法框架。

Abstract: We study discrete k-clustering problems in general metric spaces that are constrained by a combination of two different fairness conditions within the demographic fairness model. Given a metric space (P,d), where every point in P is equipped with a protected attribute, and a number k, the goal is to partition P into k clusters with a designated center each, such that a center-based objective function is minimized and the attributes are fairly distributed with respect to the following two fairness concepts: 1) group fairness: We aim for clusters with balanced numbers of attributes by specifying lower and upper bounds for the desired attribute proportions. 2) diverse center selection: Clusters have natural representatives, i.e., their centers. We ask for a balanced set of representatives by specifying the desired number of centers to choose from each attribute.
  Dickerson, Esmaeili, Morgenstern and Zhang (2023) denote the combination of these two constraints as doubly constrained fair clustering. They present algorithms whose guarantees depend on the best known approximation factors for either of these problems. Currently, this implies an 8-approximation with a small additive violation on the group fairness constraint. For k-center, we improve this approximation factor to 4 with a small additive violation. This guarantee also depends on the currently best algorithm for DS-fair k-center given by Jones, Nguyen and Nguyen (2020). For k-median and k-means, we propose the first constant-factor approximation algorithms. Our algorithms transform a solution that satisfies diverse center selection into a doubly constrained fair clustering using an LP-based approach. Furthermore, our results are generalizable to other center-selection constraints, such as matroid k-clustering and knapsack constraints.

</details>


### [100] [Fast and Memory Efficient Multimodal Journey Planning with Delays](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16149)
*Denys Katkalo,Andrii Rohovyi,Toby Walsh*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 本文优化扩展了现有的多模式路径规划算法（ULTRA、CSA、RAPTOR），使其更节省内存、更快速、更准确，特别是在处理延迟场景时性能显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有最先进的 multimodal journey-planning 算法（如ULTRA）在处理延迟问题时存在内存效率低、速度慢或准确性不足的问题，需要进行优化扩展。

Method: 对现有算法（ULTRA、CSA、RAPTOR）进行优化改进，提升其内存效率和计算速度，并扩展框架以支持延迟建模。

Result: 单标准搜索实现1.9-4.2倍加速，多标准搜索中获得竞争力的加速效果且准确性更高，并在大延迟场景下表现出更好的可扩展性。

Conclusion: 该方法在单标准搜索中实现1.9-4.2倍加速，在多标准搜索中获得竞争力的加速效果和更高准确性，且随延迟增加展现出更好的可扩展性。

Abstract: State-of-the-art multimodal journey-planning algorithms, such as ULTRA, have recently been adapted to account for delays. In this work, we extend this approach to be more memory-efficient, faster, and accurate. We also adapt this framework to other state-of-the-art algorithms, like CSA and RAPTOR. We demonstrate a speedup of 1.9-4.2x over existing algorithms in the single-criterion search. In the multicriteria setting, we achieve competitive speedup results but greater accurateness. We also found that our method scales much better as the delay increases.

</details>


### [101] [Parallelizing the branch-and-bound with isomorphism pruning algorithm for classifying orthogonal arrays](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16271)
*Dursun Bulutoglu*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种并行化分支定界同构剪枝算法的方法，并将其应用于正交阵列的分类，获得了线性加速比，并首次完成了 OA(192, k, 2, 4)（k=9,10,11）的分类。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有分支定界同构剪枝算法（Margot, 2007）在处理大规模正交阵列分类问题时存在计算瓶颈，需要通过并行化方法提升效率以完成更具挑战性的分类任务。

Method: 对 Margot 提出的分支定界同构剪枝算法进行并行化改造，用于正交阵列的分类问题。

Result: 对 OA(128, 9, 2, 4) 和 OA(144, 9, 2, 4) 的分类实现了线性加速比；首次完成了 OA(192, k, 2, 4)（k=9,10,11）的非 OD-等价分类。

Conclusion: 所提出的并行化方法能有效提升分支定界同构剪枝算法的计算效率，可成功解决更大规模正交阵列的分类问题。

Abstract: We provide a method for parallelizing the branch-and-bound with isomorphism pruning algorithm developed by Margot [Symmetric ILP: Coloring and small integers, Discrete Optimization (4) (2007), 40-62]. We apply our method to classify orthogonal arrays. For classifying all non-OD- equivalent OA(128, 9, 2, 4) and OA(144, 9, 2, 4) our method results in linear speedups. Finally, our method enables classifying all non-OD-equivalent OA(192, k, 2, 4) for k = 9, 10, 11 for the first time.

</details>


<div id='cs.MA'></div>

# cs.MA [[Back]](#toc)

### [102] [InfoChess: A Game of Adversarial Inference and a Laboratory for Quantifiable Information Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15373)
*Kieran A. Murphy*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: InfoChess是一种去除棋子吃子机制的对称对抗游戏，玩家通过移动棋子改变视野来推断对手国王位置，以此作为主要得分方式，旨在成为研究多智能体部分可观测推理的测试平台。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统国际象棋中，棋子吃子机制会干扰信息获取的核心目标；InfoChess通过消除物质激励，使竞争性信息获取成为唯一目标，从而为研究多智能体推理提供一个纯净的实验环境。

Method: 1) 设计层次化启发式智能体（基于不同水平的对手建模）；2) 训练强化学习智能体超越基线；3) 使用信息论度量（信念熵、oracle交叉熵、预测对数得分）分析游戏。

Result: RL智能体在性能上优于各层次启发式基线；信息论方法能够有效区分认知不确定性、校准偏差和对抗移动引起的不确定性。

Conclusion: InfoChess可作为研究多智能体部分可观测推理的理想测试平台，代码和公共接口已开源以供进一步研究。

Abstract: We propose InfoChess, a symmetric adversarial game that elevates competitive information acquisition to the primary objective. There is no piece capture, removing material incentives that would otherwise confound the role of information. Instead, pieces are used to alter visibility. Players are scored on their probabilistic inference of the opponent's king location over the duration of the game. To explore the space of strategies for playing InfoChess, we introduce a hierarchy of heuristic agents defined by increasing levels of opponent modeling, and train a reinforcement learning agent that outperforms these baselines. Leveraging the discrete structure of the game, we analyze gameplay through natural information-theoretic characterizations that include belief entropy, oracle cross entropy, and predictive log score under the action-induced observation channel. These measures disentangle epistemic uncertainty, calibration mismatch, and uncertainty induced by adversarial movement. The design of InfoChess renders it a testbed for studying multi-agent inference under partial observability. We release code for the environment and agents, and a public interface to encourage further study.

</details>


### [103] [Scalable Algorithms with Provable Optimality Bounds for the Multiple Watchman Route Problem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15610)
*Srikar Gouru,Ariel Felner,Jiaoyang Li*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文研究多看守路径问题（MWRP），旨在为M个看守者找到路径以覆盖地图上所有位置。提出了状态空间缩减方法和启发式技术改进，结合约束规划开发了MWRP-CP3最优规划器，以及带质量保证的次优算法MxWA*。实验表明该方法可将搜索空间缩减95%以上，运行速度提升200倍以上，并能求解更大规模的地图。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多看守路径问题（MWP）在安保、监控、环境监测等领域有重要应用。然而，现有最优算法存在计算效率低下的问题，难以扩展到大规模地图。研究动机在于解决传统方法搜索空间过大、计算时间过长、无法处理大尺寸地图的局限性。

Method: 本文方法包括三个层面：1）状态空间缩减：通过剪枝技术去除"在前往其他区域途中必然被看到的区域"；2）启发式技术改进：提升现有启发式的质量和计算效率；3）算法设计：MWRP-CP3结合约束规划与改进启发式，MxWA*是加权A*的广义变体适用于制造时间问题，同时提出anytime变体和分解子问题技术。

Result: 实验结果表明：1）MWRP-CP3相比现有最优算法搜索空间减少超过95%，运行速度快200倍以上；2）次优算法能够求解比MWRP-CP3大3倍的地图；3）所提方法在2D网格地图上验证有效，并提供开源代码和视频演示。

Conclusion: 本文通过多层次优化策略有效解决了MWRP的计算效率问题。最优规划器MWRP-CP3在大规模问题上显著优于现有方法，而次优算法通过质量边界保证进一步扩展了问题求解规模。未来工作可探索3D场景扩展和动态环境适应。

Abstract: In this paper, we tackle the Multiple Watchman Route Problem (MWRP), which aims to find a set of paths that M watchmen can follow such that every location on the map can be seen by at least one watchman. First, we propose multiple methods to reduce the state space over which a search needs to be conducted by pruning map areas that are guaranteed to be seen en route to other areas. Next, we introduce MWRP-CP3, an efficient optimal planner that combines these methods with techniques that improve the quality and calculation time of existing heuristics. We present several suboptimal algorithms with bounds on solution quality, including MxWA*, a general variant of weighted A* for makespan problems. We also present anytime variations of our suboptimal algorithms, as well as techniques to improve an existing suboptimal solution by solving multiple decomposed sub-problems. We show that MWRP-CP3 can reduce the search space by more than 95% and runs more than 200x faster than existing optimal algorithms on 2D grid maps. We also show that our suboptimal algorithms solve maps 3x larger than those solvable by MWRP-CP3. See mwrp-cp3.github.io for the open source codebase and video demonstrations.

</details>


### [104] [AstroVLM: Expert Multi-agent Collaborative Reasoning for Astronomical Imaging Quality Diagnosis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16024)
*Yaohui Han,Tianshuo Wang,Zixi Zhao,Zhengchun Zhu,Shuo Ren,Yiru Wang,Rongliang Fu,Tinghuan Chen,Tsung-Yi Ho*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: AstroVLM是一个用于天文图像质量诊断的协作式多智能体系统，利用视觉语言模型处理天文成像这类复杂多过程任务，在真实天文图像质量诊断任务中优于所有基线模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 天文成像过程涉及多学科知识和多个子任务，过程间存在复杂的底层关联，导致天文图像的质量诊断和错误定位极具挑战性。NASA等世界顶级天文组织和专业爱好者都需要投入大量时间精力。当前缺乏专门针对天文成像这一复杂领域的多智能体视觉语言模型研究。

Method: 提出AstroVLM，一个协作式多智能体系统，整合视觉语言模型能力专门用于诊断天文图像质量。系统通过多智能体协作机制处理天文成像的复杂多过程任务。

Result: 实验结果表明，AstroVLM在真实世界天文图像质量诊断任务中优于所有基线模型，展现了语言模型处理复杂多过程任务的能力。

Conclusion: AstroVLM为语言模型处理复杂多过程任务提供了参考范式，证明了多智能体协作系统在天文成像质量诊断领域的有效性和应用潜力。

Abstract: Vision Language Models (VLMs) have been applied to several specific domains and have shown strong problem-solving capabilities. However, astronomical imaging, a quite complex problem involving multidisciplinary knowledge and several subtasks, has not been adequately studied. Due to the complexity of the astronomical imaging process, both world-class astronomical organizations, such as NASA, and expert enthusiasts devote a great deal of time and effort. This is because the processes in astronomical imaging have complex underlying correlations that significantly influence one another, making the quality diagnosis and error localization of astronomical images challenging. To address this problem, we propose AstroVLM, a collaborative multi-agent system for diagnosing the quality of astronomical images. Experiment results show that AstroVLM outperforms all baselines on real-world astronomical imaging quality diagnosis tasks, providing a reference for language models to handle complicated multi-process tasks.

</details>


### [105] [Veritas-RPM: Provenance-Guided Multi-Agent False Positive Suppression for Remote Patient Monitoring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16081)
*Aswini Misro,Vikash Sharma,Shreyank N Gowda*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: Veritas-RPM is a provenance-guided multi-agent system designed to reduce false positives in remote patient monitoring by using a hierarchical architecture of 5 processing layers and a synthetic taxonomy of 98 false-positive scenarios to process 530 synthetic patient epochs, with performance measured via TSR, FER, and INDR metrics.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: False positives in Remote Patient Monitoring (RPM) systems create alert fatigue and reduce clinical efficiency, necessitating intelligent filtering mechanisms.

Method: Multi-agent architecture with 5 processing layers (VeritasAgent, SentinelLayer, DirectorAgent, Specialist Agents, MetaSentinelAgent) combined with a 98-case synthetic taxonomy of false-positive patterns to generate and process 530 synthetic patient epochs.

Result: Achieved quantifiable True Suppression Rate (TSR), False Escalation Rate (FER), and Indeterminate Rate (INDR) performance metrics using 530 synthetic patient epochs with known ground-truth labels.

Conclusion: Not explicitly stated in the abstract; presumably achieves improved balance between suppressing true RPM alerts and reducing false escalations compared to baseline methods.

Abstract: We present Veritas-RPM, a provenance-guided multi-agent architecture comprising five processing layers: VeritasAgent (ground-truth assembly), SentinelLayer (anomaly detection), DirectorAgent (specialist routing), six domain Specialist Agents, and MetaSentinelAgent (conflict resolution and final decision). We construct a 98-case synthetic taxonomy of false-positive scenarios derived from documented RPM patterns. Synthetic patient epochs (n = 530) were generated directly from taxonomy parameters and processed through the pipeline. Ground-truth labels are known for all cases. Performance is reported as True Suppression Rate (TSR), False Escalation Rate (FER), and Indeterminate Rate (INDR).

</details>


<div id='cs.DC'></div>

# cs.DC [[Back]](#toc)

### [106] [DataCenterGym: A Physics-Grounded Simulator for Multi-Objective Data Center Scheduling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15594)
*Nilavra Pathak,Samadrita Biswas,Nirmalya Roy*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: DataCenterGym是一个基于物理的地理分布式数据中心作业调度仿真环境，集成了计算排队、建筑热 dynamics、HVAC行为和温度依赖的服务降级，并在Gymnasium兼容接口中实现。同时提出了分层模型预测控制（H-MPC）调度算法，能够在考虑热力和电力动态的情况下进行分布式作业放置。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有调度器将计算利用、热生成、冷却需求和能耗等效应独立处理，但实际上这些因素紧密耦合。现有调度器缺乏能够统一建模这些耦合关系的可重用测试平台。

Method: 1) 构建物理基础的仿真环境DataCenterGym，集成了计算排队、建筑热动力学、局部HVAC行为和温度依赖的服务降级；2) 开发H-MPC调度算法，进行分布式作业放置同时显式考虑热力和电力动态。

Result: 通过名义运行和负载敏感性的实验，验证了H-MPC相对于基线调度器在调度性能上的提升。

Conclusion: DataCenterGym为地理分布式数据中心的作业调度研究提供了一个可重用的测试平台，H-MPC算法能够有效处理复杂的耦合动态问题。

Abstract: Modern datacenters schedule heterogeneous workloads across geo-distributed sites with diverse compute capacities, electricity prices, and thermal conditions. Compute utilization, heat generation, cooling demand, and energy consumption are tightly coupled, yet most existing schedulers abstract these effects and treat them independently.
  We present \textit{DataCenterGym}, a physics-grounded simulation environment for job scheduling in geo-distributed data centers, designed as a reusable testbed for future research. The simulator integrates compute queueing, building thermal dynamics, localized HVAC behavior, and temperature-dependent service degradation within a Gymnasium-compatible interface. We also develop a Hierarchical Model Predictive Control (H-MPC) scheduling algorithm that performs distributed job placement while explicitly accounting for thermal and power dynamics. Through experiments on nominal operation and workload sensitivity, we demonstrate how H-MPC improves scheduling performance relative to baseline schedulers.

</details>


### [107] [BlockRaFT: A Distributed Framework for Fault-Tolerant and Scalable Blockchain Nodes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15731)
*Manaswini Piduguralla,Souvik Sarkar,Arunmoezhi Ramachandran,Sathya Peri*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: Summary generation failed


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: Blockchain technology enhances transparency by maintaining a distributed ledger among mutually untrusting parties. Despite its advantages, scalability and availability remain critical bottlenecks that hinder widespread adoption. The increasing complexity of blockchain nodes further necessitates robust fault tolerance and high throughput to ensure seamless operations.
  We present BlockRaFT, a crash-tolerant distributed framework designed to improve both the scalability and reliability of blockchain node operations. BlockRaFT framework utilizes RAFT consensus protocol to elect a leader within a cluster of systems. The elected leader coordinates and distributes workloads across follower nodes, thereby optimizing resource utilization and work load balancing. We analyzed the tasks performed by blockchain nodes and partition them according to their stateful and stateless characteristics. Stateless operations are centralized at the leader, while stateful operations are replicated and coordinated across the cluster to ensure consistency and fault tolerance. We evaluate whether this distributed intra-node architecture provides measurable benefits over traditional single-node execution models in terms of scalability, availability, and performance.
  Additionally, we introduce a concurrent Merkle tree optimization that decouples smart contract execution from tree updates, significantly reducing one of the significant performance overheads in blockchain systems. Our design philosophy is rooted in utilizing the well-established principles of distributed computing and customizing them for the blockchain domain rather than reinventing them.

</details>


### [108] [Accuracy Is Speed: Towards Long-Context-Aware Routing for Distributed LLM Serving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15732)
*Takeshi Yoshimura,Valentijn Dymphnus van de Beek,Tatsuhiro Chiba*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文研究分布式LLM服务系统中长上下文工作负载下的准确性问题，提出TTCA指标衡量获取正确答案的耗时，并证明在长上下文场景下准确率直接影响系统速度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有分布式LLM服务系统仅关注单次请求的延迟和吞吐量，忽视了长上下文工作负载下推理准确率的变异性。当响应错误触发重试时，用户实际感知的延迟会累积增加，但传统指标无法捕捉这一现象。

Method: 1. 提出TTCA（Time-to-Correct-Answer）指标，测量获得首个正确答案的墙上时间；2. 通过测量研究揭示prompt长度和语言如何放大准确率方差；3. 设计LAAR（轻量级准确率感知路由）机制，实现基于能力的路由分发。

Result: 实验表明prompt特征（长度、语言）会显著放大准确率方差，从而增加TTCA；LAAR通过能力感知的路由设计有效降低了TTCA。

Conclusion: 在长上下文分布式服务场景中，准确率应被视为第一优先级的系统优化目标。

Abstract: Distributed LLM serving systems optimize per-request latency and throughput. However, under long-context workloads, inference accuracy becomes more variable. When incorrect responses trigger retries, accuracy directly translates into cumulative user-visible delay that is not captured by single-shot latency metrics.
  In this work, we argue that under long-context serving, \textbf{accuracy becomes speed} through retry dynamics. We introduce \textit{Time-to-Correct-Answer (TTCA)}, a metric that measures the wall-clock time required to obtain the first correct response. Our measurement study shows that prompt characteristics such as length and language amplify accuracy variance, which inflates TTCA. We demonstrate \textit{Lightweight Accuracy-Aware Routing (LAAR)}, a capability-based routing design that reduces TTCA. Our results suggest that in long-context distributed serving, accuracy should be treated as a first-class systems objective.

</details>


### [109] [cuNNQS-SCI: A Fully GPU-Accelerated Framework for High-Performance Configuration Interaction Selection withNeural Network QQantum States](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15768)
*Daran Sun,Bowen Kan,Haoquan Long,Hairui Zhao,Haoxu Li,Yicheng Liu,Pengyu Zhou,Ankang Feng,Wenjing Huang,Yida Gu,Zhenyu Li,Honghui Shang,Yunquan Zhang,Dingwen Tao,Ninghui Sun,Guangming Tan*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: cuNNQS-SCI 是一个完全GPU加速的量子化学计算框架，通过分布式去重算法、专用CUDA内核和GPU内存运行时管理，突破了原有NNQS-SCI方法的CPU瓶颈限制，在64 GPU集群上实现2.32倍加速且保持化学精度，并维持90%以上的并行效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有NNQS-SCI方法在求解多体系统薛定谔方程时存在严重的可扩展性瓶颈：集中式CPU全局去重造成通信瓶颈，宿主端耦合构型生成产生巨大计算开销，限制了其在更大系统上的应用。

Method: 提出三大技术创新：1) 分布式负载均衡全局去重算法，最小化冗余和通信开销；2) 专用细粒度CUDA内核用于精确耦合构型生成；3) GPU内存中心运行时，包含池化、流式小批次和重叠卸载策略以突破单GPU内存限制。

Result: 在64块NVIDIA A100 GPU集群上，相比高度优化的NNQS-SCI基线实现端到端2.32倍加速，且保持相同化学精度；强扩展测试中维持超过90%的并行效率。

Conclusion: cuNNQS-SCI从根本上扩展了可求解问题的规模，将性能瓶颈从主机侧限制转移回设备端推理，使得更大配置空间的问题得以解决。

Abstract: AI-driven methods have demonstrated considerable success in tackling the central challenge of accurately solving the Schrödinger equation for complex many-body systems. Among neural network quantum state (NNQS) approaches, the NNQS-SCI (Selected Configuration Interaction) method stands out as a state-of-the-art technique, recognized for its high accuracy and scalability. However, its application to larger systems is severely constrained by a hybrid CPU-GPU architecture. Specifically, centralized CPU-based global de-duplication creates a severe scalability barrier due to communication bottlenecks, while host-resident coupled-configuration generation induces prohibitive computational overheads. We introduce cuNNQS-SCI, a fully GPU-accelerated SCI framework designed to overcome these bottlenecks. cuNNQS-SCI first integrates a distributed, load-balanced global de-duplication algorithm to minimize redundancy and communication overhead at scale. To address compute limitations, it employs specialized, fine-grained CUDA kernels for exact coupled configuration generation. Finally, to break the single-GPU memory barrier exposed by this full acceleration, it incorporates a GPU memory-centric runtime featuring GPU-side pooling, streaming mini-batches, and overlapped offloading. This design enables much larger configuration spaces and shifts the bottleneck from host-side limitations back to on-device inference. Our evaluation demonstrates that cuNNQS-SCI fundamentally expands the scale of solvable problems. On an NVIDIA A100 cluster with 64 GPUs, cuNNQS-SCI achieves up to 2.32X end-to-end speedup over the highly-optimized NNQS-SCI baseline while preserving the same chemical accuracy. Furthermore, it demonstrates excellent distributed performance, maintaining over 90% parallel efficiency in strong scaling tests.

</details>


### [110] [CroSatFL: Energy-Efficient Federated Learning with Cross-Aggregation for Satellite Edge Computing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15779)
*Nan Yang,Bahman Javadi,Rodrigo Neves Calheiros,David Boland,Philip Leong*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: CroSatFL是一种面向LEO卫星星座的层级联邦学习框架，通过将地面站从迭代循环中解放出来、利用激光星间链路进行迭代交换，并集成StarMask聚类、Skip-One跳过机制和random-k交叉聚合三个能量感知机制，显著降低了地面站通信和传输能耗，同时保持了良好的训练性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低地球轨道（LEO）巨型星座将云-边缘连续体扩展至太空，催生卫星边缘计算。然而，该环境下的联邦学习面临根本性的能量约束挑战：动态的星间连接、异构的机载计算硬件以及严格的功率预算，限制了传统联邦学习方案的适用性。

Method: 提出CroSatFL，一种可持续的轨道上层级联邦学习框架，包含三个能量感知机制：(1) StarMask形成满足激光星间链路约束的聚类，将数据量与异构CPU/GPU能力对齐；(2) Skip-One通过跳过最多一个慢速客户端来降低轮次能耗和延迟，同时保持长期公平性；(3) random-k交叉聚合实现轻量级拓扑感知的跨聚类混合。采用端到端能量模型和Walker-Delta星座进行评估。

Result: 实验表明， CroSatFL相比地面站中心基线和轨道基线，地面站通信次数减少超过两个数量级，地面站传输能耗降低约6倍，同时实现了有竞争力的精度和更快的收敛速度。

Conclusion: CroSatFL通过将地面站从迭代循环中解放出来，仅需两次通信（初始化和最终模型收集），大幅降低了对带宽受限且能耗昂贵的地面站链路的依赖，同时利用激光星间链路实现轻量级迭代交换，在保证精度的前提下实现了显著的能量节省。

Abstract: Low Earth Orbit (LEO) mega-constellations extend the cloud-to-edge continuum into space, enabling satellite edge computing. However, Federated Learning (FL) in this environment is fundamentally energy-constrained due to dynamic inter-satellite connectivity, heterogeneous onboard computing hardware, and strict power budgets. We propose CroSatFL, a sustainable on-orbit hierarchical FL framework that reduces end-to-end energy across computation and communication while maintaining strong training performance under realistic LEO dynamics. CroSatFL keeps the ground station (GS) off the iterative loop by performing all local training and intermediate aggregations on orbit, requiring only two GS communication phases: one for initialization and one for final model collection. This sharply reduces repeated use of bandwidth-limited and energy-expensive GS links and shifts iterative exchanges to laser inter-satellite links (LISLs). CroSatFL integrates three energy-aware mechanisms: StarMask forms LISL-feasible clusters that align data volume with heterogeneous CPU/GPU capability, Skip-One mitigates transient stragglers by skipping at most one slow client per cluster to lower round energy and latency while preserving long-term fairness, and random-k cross-aggregation enables lightweight topology-aware cross-cluster mixing without extending round duration. Using an end-to-end energy model with a realistic Walker-Delta constellation, we show that CroSatFL reduces GS communication count by over two orders of magnitude and GS transmission energy by about 6x relative to GS-centric and on-orbit baselines, while achieving competitive accuracy and faster convergence.

</details>


### [111] [Breaking the Training Barrier of Billion-Parameter Universal Machine Learning Interatomic Potentials](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15821)
*Yuanchang Zhou,Hongyu Wang,Yiming Du,Yan Wang,Mingzhen Li,Siyu Hu,Xiangyu Zhang,Weijian Liu,Chen Wang,Zhuoqiang Guo,Long Wang,Jingde Bu,Yutong Lu,Guangming Tan,Weile Jia*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出MatRIS-MoE（十亿参数混合专家模型）和Janus（面向uMLIPs的分布式训练框架），在两台Exascale超级计算机上实现1.2/1.0 EFLOPS的峰值性能，训练并行效率超90%，将大规模ML原子间势能模型的训练时间从数周压缩至数小时，为AI for Science基础模型树立新标杆。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: uMLIPs训练需要二阶导数，但现有缺乏对应的并行训练框架；扩展至十亿参数规模时，计算和通信开销呈爆炸式增长，导致训练极具挑战性。

Method: 提出了两个核心贡献：(1) MatRIS-MoE：基于不变架构的十亿参数混合专家模型；(2) Janus：首个面向uMLIPs的高维分布式训练框架，包含硬件感知的优化策略。该系统部署于两台百亿亿次级超级计算机。

Result: 实现单精度峰值性能1.2/1.0 EFLOPS（分别达到理论峰值的24%和35.5%），并行效率超过90%，成功将十亿参数uMLIPs的训练时间从数周缩短至数小时。

Conclusion: 本文在AI for Science领域树立了Exascale级别基础模型的新里程碑，为快速科学发现提供了关键基础设施。将十亿参数量级的uMLIPs训练周期从数周压缩至数小时。

Abstract: Universal Machine Learning Interatomic Potentials (uMLIPs), pre-trained on massively diverse datasets encompassing inorganic materials and organic molecules across the entire periodic table, serve as foundational models for quantum-accurate physical simulations. However, uMLIP training requires second-order derivatives, which lack corresponding parallel training frameworks; moreover, scaling to the billion-parameter regime causes explosive growth in computation and communication overhead, making its training a tremendous challenge. We introduce MatRIS-MoE, a billion-parameter Mixture-of-Experts model built upon invariant architecture, and {Janus}, a pioneering high-dimensional distributed training framework for uMLIPs with hardware-aware optimizations. Deployed across two Exascale supercomputers, our code attains a peak performance of 1.2/1.0 EFLOPS (24\%/{35.5\%} of theoretical peak) in single precision at over 90\% parallel efficiency, compressing the training of billion-parameter uMLIPs from weeks to hours. This work establishes a new high-water mark for AI-for-Science (AI4S) foundation models at Exascale and provides essential infrastructure for rapid scientific discovery.

</details>


### [112] [New Kids: An Architecture and Performance Investigation of Second-Generation Serverless Platforms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15916)
*Trever Schirmer,Aris Wiegand,Lucca di Benedetto,Linus Gustafsson,Natalie Carl,Tobias Pfandzelter,David Bermbach*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 该研究揭示了serverless计算平台的代际演进：第二代平台通过采用轻量级隔离和边缘部署，将请求延迟降低至10ms级并消除冷启动瓶颈，但以牺牲部分执行环境能力为代价。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着serverless计算在产业界和学术界的广泛采用，理解支撑其运行的底层平台机制变得至关重要。serverless技术已发展超过十年，不同平台采用了截然不同的技术方案，但缺乏系统性的比较分析。

Method: 研究采用文献调研与实验评估相结合的方法：1）收集并分析7个serverless平台公开的架构信息；2）基于微基准测试进行性能评估，累计执行超过38 million次函数调用。

Result: 研究发现serverless平台存在两代技术演进：第一代平台基于容器化和中心化执行架构；第二代平台则采用轻量级隔离（lightweight isolates）和边缘部署策略。新一代技术将暖请求延迟从约40ms降低至10ms左右，冷启动问题基本消除，但执行环境功能受到限制。

Conclusion: 研究揭示了Serverless平台从第一代基于容器化中心化架构向第二代基于轻量级隔离和边缘部署的演进路径。通过对7个平台38 million+次函数调用的基准测试，验证了新一代平台将暖请求延迟从40ms降至10ms左右，并将冷启动问题降至可忽略水平，但代价是执行环境的限制。该研究为理解serverless平台底层机制提供了全面深入的洞察。

Abstract: With the ever-increasing usage of serverless computing in both industry and academia, it is essential to understand the mechanisms that power the underlying platforms. As serverless is more than ten years old, there are different platforms with vastly different approaches. We show that, next to the traditional and popular platforms, a second generation of serverless platform has emerged. While first-generation platforms are based on containerized, centralized execution, the new generation leverages lightweight isolates and edge deployment. This evolution reduces warm request latency from approximately 40 ms to around 10 ms and reduces cold starts to an afterthought, but limits the execution environment. In this paper, we gather and analyze all publicly available information to provide detailed insights into the underlying architecture of seven platforms and then run a microbenchmark-based evaluation totaling more than 38 million function calls to gain a deeper understanding their performance.

</details>


### [113] [Continuous benchmarking: Keeping pace with an evolving ecosystem of models and technologies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15919)
*Jan Vogelsang,Melissa Lober,Catherine Mia Schöfmann,José Villamar,Dennis Terhorst,Johanna Senk,Hans Ekkehard Plesser,Markus Diesmann,Susanne Kunkel,Anno C. Kurth*

Abstract: Drawing on ideas from continuous integration, we present concepts of an automated benchmarking pipeline for high performance applications. Customization and collaboration have been key design goals owing to the requirements of research-software development as a continuous community effort. We have extended our previous conceptual work on systematic benchmarking workflows with the functionality of user-agnostic operations as well as continuous benchmarking. This fosters reproducibility and re-use of benchmarking results to ensure sustainable technological progress. We provide software-engineering solutions to keep pace with the rapid evolution of both large-scale models and high-performance computing systems with a view towards the scientific domains of neuroscience and artificial intelligence.

</details>


### [114] [Robust Synchronisation for Federated Learning in The Face of Correlated Device Failure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16090)
*Stefan Behfar,Richard Mortier*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 针对联邦学习中设备可用性与数据分布相关联导致的公平性采样问题，提出AW-PSP方法，通过实时可用性预测和历史行为分析动态调整节点采样概率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准PSP假设设备行为静态独立，但在边缘计算场景下，设备因移动性、功耗、用户活动等因素导致可用性异构，且可用性与数据分布可能存在关联性。这使得高可用设备主导训练、低可用设备数据被长期忽视，造成某些类别或群体特征学习不充分。

Method: 提出Availability-Weighted PSP (AW-PSP)，核心包括：(1) 基于马尔可夫的可用性预测器，区分瞬时故障与慢性故障；(2) 分布式哈希表(DHT)层去中心化管理元数据（延迟、新鲜度、效用分数）；(3) 综合实时可用性预测、历史行为和故障相关性指标动态调整采样概率。

Result: 实验结果表明AW-PSP在独立故障和相关性故障场景下均表现更强的鲁棒性，提升了标签覆盖率，降低了公平性方差。

Conclusion: AW-PSP为大规模、异构且故障频发的FL部署提供了一种可用性感知的公平节点采样协议。

Abstract: Probabilistic Synchronous Parallel (PSP) is a technique in distributed learning systems to reduce synchronization bottlenecks by sampling a subset of participating nodes per round. In Federated Learning (FL), where edge devices are often unreliable due to factors including mobility, power constraints, and user activity, PSP helps improve system throughput. However, PSP has a key limitation: it assumes device behavior is static and different devices are independent. This can lead to unfair distributed synchronization, due to highly available nodes dominating training while those that are often unavailable rarely participate and so their data may be missed. If both data distribution and node availability are simultaneously correlated with the device, then both PSP and standard FL algorithms will suffer from persistent under-representation of certain classes or groups resulting in inefficient or ineffective learning of certain features. We introduce Availability-Weighted PSP (AW-PSP), an extension to PSP that addresses the issue of co-correlation of unfair sampling and data availability by dynamically adjusting node sampling probabilities using real-time availability predictions, historical behavior, and failure correlation metrics. A Markov-based availability predictor distinguishes transient \emph{vs} chronic failures, while a Distributed Hash Table (DHT) layer decentralizes metadata, including latency, freshness, and utility scores. We implement AW-PSP and trace-driven evaluation shows that it improves robustness to both independent and correlated failures, increases label coverage, and reduces fairness variance compared to standard PSP. AW-PSP thus provides an availability-aware, and fairness-conscious node sampling protocol for FL deployments that will scale to large numbers of nodes even in heterogeneous and failure-prone environments.

</details>


### [115] [Compositional Design, Implementation, and Verification of Swarms (Technical Report)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16097)
*Florian Furbach,Lucas Clorius,Roland Kuhn,Hernán Melgratti,Alceste Scalas,Emilio Tuosto*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文针对 swarm 协议的组合性问题，提出了一套组合式规格说明、验证和实现理论与技术，并开发了配套软件工具支持独立设计组件的自动集成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 swarm 模型缺乏组合性，导致大型复杂 swarm 应用的模块化开发和代码复用困难。

Method: 提出新的组合理论和技术，用于 swarm 的组合式规格说明、验证和实现。

Result: 实现了正确组合复用既有 swarm 协议和机器实现的能力。

Conclusion: 实现了配套软件工具，支持独立设计和验证的 swarm 组件的自动集成。

Abstract: Swarm protocols are a recently introduced formalism for specifying, implementing, and verifying peer-to-peer systems called swarms. A swarm consists of distributed agents called machines that communicate by asynchronous event propagation. Following a local-first model, each machine can progress without requiring continuous connectivity to other machines. Existing models of swarms are not compositional, making the modular development of large and complex swarm applications as well as the reuse of code difficult. We address these issues by presenting novel theory and techniques for the compositional specification, verification, and implementation of swarms. These results enable the correct compositional reuse of pre-existing swarm protocols and machine implementations. We implement these contributions in a companion software artifact which enables the automatic integration of independently designed and verified swarm components.

</details>


### [116] [Logarithmic-Time Geodesically Convex Decomposition in Programmable Matter](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16112)
*Henning Hillebrandt,Andreas Padalkin,Christian Scheideler,Daniel Warner,Julian Werthmann*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: Summary generation failed


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: The decomposition of complex structures into simpler substructures is a powerful technique with a wide range of applications. We study the computation of decompositions in the context of programmable matter. The amoebot model is a well-established model for programmable matter, which places $n$ tiny robots called amoebots on the triangular grid. We consider the reconfigurable circuit extension of the geometric amoebot model, which allows amoebots to interconnect via so-called circuits. Amoebots can then instantaneously transmit simple beeps to all amoebots connected by the same circuit. Using reconfigurable circuits, previous papers have described a linear-time triangulation algorithm, and a logarithmic-time decomposition algorithm into so-called tunnel regions. Both algorithms only work on a restricted class of amoebot structures. In this paper, we define a decomposition into $O(|\mathcal H|)$ simple, geodesically convex regions for arbitrary amoebot structures, and show how it can compute such a decomposition in $O(\log n)$ rounds, where $|\mathcal H|$ denotes the number of holes in the amoebot structure. As a byproduct, we also improve the global maxima algorithm of Padalkin et al. (Nat. Comput., 2024) for special cases and with that also their spanning tree algorithm to $O(\log n)$ rounds w.h.p.

</details>

### [117] [The Spectral Geometry of Thought: Phase Transitions, Instruction Reversal, Token-Level Dynamics, and Perfect Correctness Prediction in How Transformers Reason](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15350)
*Yi Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究通过对11个模型（跨5个架构家族）进行系统谱分析，发现大型语言模型在推理与事实回忆任务中表现出七种核心谱相变现象，建立了Transformer推理的"谱理论"。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解Transformer中推理的机制基础，通过谱分析揭示推理与事实回忆在隐藏激活空间中的几何差异，并探索其作为模型能力预测指标的潜力。

Method: 对5个架构家族（Qwen, Pythia, Phi, Llama, DeepSeek-R1）的11个模型进行系统谱分析，追踪隐藏激活空间中的α参数变化，研究token级别和推理步骤级别的谱动态。

Result: 发现七种核心现象：1）推理谱压缩（9/11模型推理时α显著降低）；2）指令微调谱反转（基础模型与指令微调模型呈相反关系）；3）架构依赖的生成分类学（扩张/压缩/平衡三种模式）；4）谱标度律（α_reasoning ∝ -0.074 ln N）；5）Token级谱瀑布（层间同步指数衰减，推理任务中更弱）；6）推理步骤谱标点（相变与推理边界对齐）；7）谱正确性预测（仅用α在Qwen2.5-7B晚层达AUC=1.000，6模型平均AUC=0.893）。

Conclusion: 建立了Transformer推理的综合谱理论，揭示"思维几何"具有架构通用性方向、架构特异性动态、可预测结果三大特征，为理解LLM推理机制和预测能力提供新框架。

Abstract: We discover that large language models exhibit \emph{spectral phase transitions} in their hidden activation spaces when engaging in reasoning versus factual recall. Through systematic spectral analysis across \textbf{11 models} spanning \textbf{5 architecture families} (Qwen, Pythia, Phi, Llama, DeepSeek-R1), we identify \textbf{seven} core phenomena: (1)~\textbf{Reasoning Spectral Compression} -- 9/11 models show significantly lower $α$ for reasoning ($p < 0.05$), with larger effects in stronger models; (2)~\textbf{Instruction Tuning Spectral Reversal} -- base models show reasoning $α< $ factual $α$, while instruction-tuned models reverse this relationship; (3)~\textbf{Architecture-Dependent Generation Taxonomy} -- prompt-to-response shifts partition into expansion, compression, and equilibrium regimes; (4)~\textbf{Spectral Scaling Law} -- $α_\text{reasoning} \propto -0.074 \ln N$ across 4 Qwen base models ($R^2 = 0.46$); (5)~\textbf{Token-Level Spectral Cascade} -- per-token alpha tracking reveals local synchronization that decays exponentially with layer distance, and is weaker for reasoning than factual tasks; (6)~\textbf{Reasoning Step Spectral Punctuation} -- phase-transition signatures align with reasoning step boundaries; and (7)~\textbf{Spectral Correctness Prediction} -- spectral $α$ alone achieves AUC $= 1.000$ (Qwen2.5-7B, late layers) and mean AUC $= 0.893$ across 6 models in predicting correctness \emph{before} the final answer is generated. Together, these findings establish a comprehensive \emph{spectral theory of reasoning} in transformers, revealing that the geometry of thought is universal in direction, architecture-specific in dynamics, and predictive of outcome.

</details>


### [118] [Sequential KV Cache Compression via Probabilistic Language Tries: Beyond the Per-Vector Shannon Limit](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15356)
*Gregory Magarshak*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对Transformer KV缓存压缩问题，提出两层架构的顺序KV压缩方法，利用语言模型的概率预测能力超越现有基于标量压缩的香农熵极限。在标准困惑度下可达914倍甚至914,000倍的压缩比提升，且与现有量化方法正交可叠加。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有KV缓存量化方法（如TurboQuant）虽已接近每向量压缩的香农熵极限，但该极限实际上是一个弱约束——真实问题是压缩整个KV缓存序列而非单个向量。KV缓存中的token来自模型训练的确切形式语言，模型本身就是该语言的近似最优预测器，这一结构特性尚未被利用。

Method: 提出两层顺序KV压缩架构：(1) 概率前缀去重层——使用概率语言Trie（PLT）的度量d_T(s, s') = -log_2 P_M(s ^ s')识别跨会话的语义等价共享前缀；(2) 预测差分编码层——仅存储新KV向量相对于模型自身预测的残差。理论界：H(KV_{i+1} | KV_{<=i}) ≤ H(token_{i+1} | token_{<=i})。

Result: 在标准语言模型困惑度（10-20，流利英文文本）下，每token位置的熵界为3.3-4.3 bits，而TurboQuant每向量分量为3 bits（典型注意力头含64-128分量）。香农极限下相对TurboQuant的压缩比约914,000倍；即使在熵底线上方1000倍的悲观最坏情况下，压缩比仍约914倍，且压缩效果随上下文长度增加而提升。

Conclusion: 两层架构相互正交，可与包括TurboQuant在内的现有每向量量化方法叠加使用，突破了传统标量压缩的信息论极限，为KV缓存压缩提供了新的理论框架和实践路径。

Abstract: Recent work on KV cache quantization, culminating in TurboQuant, has approached the Shannon entropy limit for per-vector compression of transformer key-value caches. We observe that this limit applies to a strictly weaker problem than the one that actually matters: compressing the KV cache as a sequence. The tokens stored in a KV cache are not arbitrary floating-point data -- they are samples from the exact formal language the model was trained on, and the model is by construction a near-optimal predictor of that language. We introduce sequential KV compression, a two-layer architecture that exploits this structure. The first layer, probabilistic prefix deduplication, identifies semantically equivalent shared prefixes across sessions using the trie metric d_T(s, s') = -log_2 P_M(s ^ s') from Probabilistic Language Tries (PLTs). The second layer, predictive delta coding, stores only the residual of each new KV vector from the model's own prediction of it, achieving a per-token entropy bound of H(KV_{i+1} | KV_{<=i}) <= H(token_{i+1} | token_{<=i}). We prove that at typical language model perplexity -- approximately 10-20 for fluent English text -- this bound is 3.3-4.3 bits on average per token position, compared to TurboQuant's 3 bits per vector component (with typical attention heads having 64-128 components). The theoretical compression ratio over TurboQuant is approximately 914,000x at the Shannon limit. Even at 1000x above the entropy floor -- a deliberately pessimistic worst-case overhead, two orders of magnitude above the 2-5x typical of practical source coders -- the ratio remains approximately 914x over TurboQuant, with compression improving rather than degrading as context length grows. The two layers are orthogonal and compose with existing per-vector quantization methods including TurboQuant.

</details>


### [119] [Mapping High-Performance Regions in Battery Scheduling across Data Uncertainty, Battery Design, and Planning Horizons](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15360)
*Jaime de Miguel Rodriguez,Artjom Vargunin,Brigitta Robin Raudne,David Solis Martin,Yaroslava Mykhailenko,Kaarel Oja*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究通过三要素分析揭示了储能系统中有效预测范围的概念，证明考虑该范围可在保持最优性能的同时降低计算成本。研究提供了跨多参数组合的最优预测范围参考，并为工业储能运营的机器学习优化方法奠定基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在储能运营中，预测范围的选择直接影响计算成本和运营性能。需要理解数据特征、预测不确定性与最优预测范围之间的关系，以实现计算效率与最优性能的平衡。

Method: 采用三要素分析框架，结合多阶段模型预测控制，使用合成数据集系统探索数据特征和不确定性的变化。通过参数化方法构建数据特征与最优预测范围之间的关系映射。

Result: 发现了"有效预测范围"现象——超过此范围后，增加预测信息提供的运营收益有限。提供了不同电池类型、不确定性水平和数据特征组合下的最优预测范围。量化了预测不确定性导致的收入损失，表明误差即使对快速电池也会影响性能。

Conclusion: 提供了一个可扩展的框架，为工业储能运营提供实际指导，并为未来机器学习方法奠定基础，可将数据集参数化映射到最优预测范围，实现持续优化而无需大量计算。

Abstract: This study presents a triadic analysis of energy storage operation under multi-stage model predictive control, investigating the interplay between data characteristics, forecast uncertainty, planning horizon, and battery c-rate. Synthetic datasets are generated to systematically explore variations in data profiles and uncertainty, enabling parametrization and the construction of relationships that map these characteristics to optimal horizon length. Results reveal the presence of an effective horizon, defined as the look-ahead length beyond which additional forecast information provides limited operational benefit. Accounting for this horizon can reduce computational costs while maintaining optimal performance. The study provides optimal horizon lengths across a broad range of combinations of battery types, uncertainty levels, and data profiles, offering practical guidance for industrial storage operation. It also quantifies revenue losses due to forecast uncertainty, showing that errors can impact performance even for fast batteries. Finally, the framework lays the groundwork for future machine learning approaches that map dataset parametrization to optimal horizons, supporting continuous optimization in industrial settings without heavy computation.

</details>


### [120] [M3R: Localized Rainfall Nowcasting with Meteorology-Informed MultiModal Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15377)
*Sanjeev Panta,Rhett M Morvant,Xu Yuan,Li Chen,Nian-Feng Tzeng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: M3R是一种基于多模态注意力机制的气象预测架构，通过融合NEXRAD雷达图像与气象站时间序列数据，实现高准确度的降雨即时预报，在三个实验区域均优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有深度学习方法在降雨预测中存在局限性，无法有效整合多源多媒体数据（如雷达图像与气象站数据），导致预测精度受限。

Method: 1) 构建异构气象数据的时间对齐Pipeline；2) 设计多模态注意力机制，以气象站时间序列为Query，选择性提取雷达图像中的降水特征；3) 多区域验证（100km×100km范围）。

Result: M3R在准确率、效率和降水检测能力三个方面均优于现有方法，为多媒体降水预测建立了新的基准。

Conclusion: 该研究为基于多媒体的降水即时预报提供了新的技术方案，具有实际应用于气象预测系统的潜力。

Abstract: Accurate and timely rainfall nowcasting is crucial for disaster mitigation and water resource management. Despite recent advances in deep learning, precipitation prediction remains challenging due to limitations in effectively leveraging diverse multimedia data sources. We introduce M3R, a Meteorology-informed MultiModal attention-based architecture for direct Rainfall prediction that synergistically combines visual NEXRAD radar imagery with numerical Personal Weather Station (PWS) measurements, using a comprehensive pipeline for temporal alignment of heterogeneous meteorological data. With specialized multimodal attention mechanisms, M3R novelly leverages weather station time series as queries to selectively attend to spatial radar features, enabling focused extraction of precipitation signatures. Experimental results for three spatial areas of 100 km * 100 km centered at NEXRAD radar stations demonstrate that M3R outperforms existing approaches, achieving substantial improvements in accuracy, efficiency, and precipitation detection capabilities. Our work establishes new benchmarks for multimedia-based precipitation nowcasting and provides practical tools for operational weather prediction systems. The source code is available at https://github.com/Sanjeev97/M3Rain

</details>


### [121] [Hallucination as Trajectory Commitment: Causal Evidence for Asymmetric Attractor Dynamics in Transformer Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15400)
*G. Aytug Akarlar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究表明大语言模型幻觉是由非对称吸引子动力学支配的早期轨迹承诺现象。通过同提示分叉法隔离轨迹动态，发现幻觉在首 token 即分叉，且存在显著因果不对称性（注入幻觉激活易导致错误，反向恢复困难）；修正需多步干预，而幻觉的出现可通过第0步的regime聚类结构预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决大语言模型幻觉问题的核心在于理解其因果机制，而非仅描述相关性。现有方法混淆了提示层面的混淆因素与轨迹动态，需要一种方法能隔离轨迹级现象。

Method: 采用同提示分叉法（same-prompt bifurcation）隔离轨迹动态；跨28层进行激活 patching 和窗口 patching；探测第0步残差状态；使用无监督聚类识别regime结构。使用Qwen2.5-1.5B模型，在61个提示六个类别上验证。

Result: 44.3%的提示在首 token 处发生分叉；激活注入存在显著因果不对称（注入幻觉激活导致87.5% Corruption vs 反向仅33.3%恢复）；修正需要持续多步干预而腐败仅需单次扰动；第0步残差状态可预测幻觉率（r=0.776）；无监督聚类识别5个regime组，其中saddle-adjacent簇集中了13个分叉错误前提提示中的12个。

Conclusion: 幻觉是一种局部稳定的吸引子盆地：进入是概率性的且快速，但退出需要跨层和跨步骤的协调干预。相关吸引子盆地在第0步就已可通过聚类识别，表明错误信息的根源在提示编码阶段就已确立。

Abstract: We present causal evidence that hallucination in autoregressive language models is an early trajectory commitment governed by asymmetric attractor dynamics. Using same-prompt bifurcation, in which we repeatedly sample identical inputs to observe spontaneous divergence, we isolate trajectory dynamics from prompt-level confounds. On Qwen2.5-1.5B across 61 prompts spanning six categories, 27 prompts (44.3%) bifurcate with factual and hallucinated trajectories diverging at the first generated token (KL = 0 at step 0, KL > 1.0 at step 1). Activation patching across 28 layers reveals a pronounced causal asymmetry: injecting a hallucinated activation into a correct trajectory corrupts output in 87.5% of trials (layer 20), while the reverse recovers only 33.3% (layer 24); both exceed the 10.4% baseline (p = 0.025) and 12.5% random-patch control. Window patching shows correction requires sustained multi-step intervention, whereas corruption needs only a single perturbation. Probing the prompt encoding itself, step-0 residual states predict per-prompt hallucination rate at Pearson r = 0.776 at layer 15 (p < 0.001 against a 1000-permutation null); unsupervised clustering identifies five regime-like groups (eta^2 = 0.55) whose saddle-adjacent cluster concentrates 12 of the 13 bifurcating false-premise prompts, indicating that the basin structure is organized around regime commitments fixed at prompt encoding. These findings characterize hallucination as a locally stable attractor basin: entry is probabilistic and rapid, exit demands coordinated intervention across layers and steps, and the relevant basins are selected by clusterable regimes already discernible at step 0.

</details>


### [122] [Dispatch-Aware Ragged Attention for Pruned Vision Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15408)
*Saif Mahmoud,Ahmad Almasri*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对Vision Transformers中token剪枝后注意力延迟未按预期降低的问题，发现瓶颈在于短序列下的dispatch开销（60-90μs），提出了一种轻量级双向Triton注意力内核（dispatch开销约40μs），在完整pipeline中实现最高2.24倍端到端吞吐量提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Token剪枝方法声称可实现二次方量级的注意力FLOPs降低，但当使用FlashAttention-2 varlen和PyTorch NestedTensor SDPA等先进变长注意力API时，wall-clock注意力延迟并未相应缩减，导致剪枝的效率优势无法在实测性能中体现。

Method: 识别出dispatch开销瓶颈（post-pruning序列长度<=197 tokens时，实际矩阵运算仅需个位数微秒，但host端dispatch路径消耗60-90μs）；设计轻量级双向Triton注意力内核，将dispatch开销降低至约40μs（比FlashAttention-2 varlen低约1.5倍）；集成pack-attend-unpack完整流程。

Result: 相比填充式PyTorch SDPA实现，端到端吞吐量提升最高2.24倍；在四种剪枝算法（Threshold-L2、DynamicViT、EViT、ATS）上表现一致；在DeiT-T/S/B三种模型规模上均有效扩展；分类预测保持bit-exact（最大logit差异<0.007）。

Conclusion: 通过定制低开销注意力内核，可有效释放token剪枝的效率潜力，实现实际部署中的性能收益。

Abstract: Token pruning methods for Vision Transformers (ViTs) promise quadratic reductions in attention FLOPs by dropping uninformative patches. Yet when pruned sequences are executed with state-of-the-art variable-length attention APIs -- including FlashAttention-2's varlen and PyTorch's NestedTensor SDPA-the wall-clock attention latency doesn't scale accordingly. We trace this to a dispatch-overhead bottleneck: at the short, post-pruning sequence lengths typical of ViTs (<=197 tokens), actual matrix arithmetic completes in single-digit microseconds while the host-side dispatch path consumes 60-90 us. We present a lightweight, bidirectional Triton attention kernel whose dispatch floor is 40 us roughly 1.5x lower than FlashAttention-2 varlen-allowing pruning savings to become more visible in wall-clock time. Integrated into a complete pack-attend-unpack pipeline, our system achieves up to 2.24x end-to-end throughput over padded PyTorch SDPA consistently across four pruning algorithms (Threshold-L2, DynamicViT, EViT, ATS), scales across DeiT-T/S/B, and maintains bit-exact classification predictions with <0.007 max absolute logit difference.

</details>


### [123] [Beyond Single-Model Optimization: Preserving Plasticity in Continual Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15414)
*Lute Lillo,Nick Cheney*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: TeLAPA通过质量多样性启发的策略归档方法，在共享潜在空间内维护行为多样的策略邻域，克服了传统单策略持续强化学习的"可塑性丧失"问题，实现更高效的任务学习和快速恢复。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有持续强化学习方法依赖单策略保留机制，在任务干扰后先前成功的策略无法成为可靠的快速适应起点，表现出"可塑性丧失"问题，单策略保存无法解决这一挑战。

Method: 提出TeLAPA框架：1) 将行为多样的策略邻域组织为每任务档案；2) 维持共享潜在空间使归档策略可比较和复用；3) 将持续RL从保留孤立解转变为维护"技能对齐邻域"。实验在MiniGrid持续学习环境中进行。

Result: TeLAPA能够学习更多任务、在干扰后更快恢复已访问任务能力、在任务序列上保持更高性能。分析发现：源最优策略往往不是迁移最优的，有效复用依赖于保留和选择多个邻近替代方案而非压缩为单一代表。

Conclusion: 持续RL应围绕可复用且有能力的策略邻域重新构建，为超越单模型保存、迈向更具可塑性的终身智能体提供了新路径。有效的策略复用需要在局部邻域内保留多个候选而非收敛到单一策略。

Abstract: Continual reinforcement learning must balance retention with adaptation, yet many methods still rely on \emph{single-model preservation}, committing to one evolving policy as the main reusable solution across tasks. Even when a previously successful policy is retained, it may no longer provide a reliable starting point for rapid adaptation after interference, reflecting a form of \emph{loss of plasticity} that single-policy preservation cannot address. Inspired by quality-diversity methods, we introduce \textsc{TeLAPA} (Transfer-Enabled Latent-Aligned Policy Archives), a continual RL framework that organizes behaviorally diverse policy neighborhoods into per-task archives and maintains a shared latent space so that archived policies remain comparable and reusable under non-stationary drift. This perspective shifts continual RL from retaining isolated solutions to maintaining \emph{skill-aligned neighborhoods} with competent and behaviorally related policies that support future relearning. In our MiniGrid CL setting, \textsc{TeLAPA} learns more tasks successfully, recovers competence faster on revisited tasks after interference, and retains higher performance across a sequence of tasks. Our analyses show that source-optimal policies are often not transfer-optimal, even within a local competent neighborhood, and that effective reuse depends on retaining and selecting among multiple nearby alternatives rather than collapsing them to one representative. Together, these results reframe continual RL around reusable and competent policy neighborhoods, providing a route beyond single-model preservation toward more plastic lifelong agents.

</details>


### [124] [StoSignSGD: Unbiased Structural Stochasticity Fixes SignSGD for Training Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15416)
*Dingzhi Yu,Rui Pan,Yuxing Liu,Tong Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出StoSignSGD算法，通过在符号算子中注入结构化随机性来解决SignSGD在非光滑目标函数上的不收敛问题，同时保持更新步骤的无偏性。理论上在凸优化 regime 可达到匹配下界的收敛速率，在非凸非光滑优化中通过广义平稳性测度改进了维度相关复杂度界。实验表明该算法在FP8低精度预训练和7B大模型微调任务中均显著优于AdamW和SignSGD。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SignSGD虽在分布式学习和大规模基础模型训练中表现优异，但存在根本性缺陷：在非光滑目标函数上发散不收敛。由于ReLU、最大池化、专家混合模型等组件在现代机器学习中广泛存在，非光滑目标函数无处不在，这一缺陷严重限制了SignSGD的实用性。

Method: 核心创新：在符号算子中注入结构化随机性（structural stochasticity），同时严格保持更新步骤的无偏性。额外贡献：开发了符号转换框架，可将任意通用优化器转换为无偏的符号基版本。

Result: 理论成果：①凸优化 regime 下收敛速率匹配下界；②非凸非光滑优化中引入广义平稳性测度，复杂度界相对先前最佳结果改进了维度因子。实验成果：①FP8低精度预训练场景（AdamW完全失效）保持高度稳定，相比基线实现1.44×至2.14×加速；②7B参数LLM数学推理微调任务中，相较AdamW和SignSGD均有显著性能提升。

Conclusion: StoSignSGD成功解决了SignSGD的非收敛问题，在理论和实践层面均验证了有效性。通过符号转换框架和解构实验，全面揭示了算法成功的设计要素，为后续符号基优化器研究提供了方法论基础。

Abstract: Sign-based optimization algorithms, such as SignSGD, have garnered significant attention for their remarkable performance in distributed learning and training large foundation models. Despite their empirical superiority, SignSGD is known to diverge on non-smooth objectives, which are ubiquitous in modern machine learning due to ReLUs, max-pools, and mixture-of-experts.
